### [1] [SCALE-Sim TPU: Validating and Extending SCALE-Sim for TPUs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22535)
*Jingtian Dang,Ritik Raj,Changhai Man,Jianming Tong,Tushar Krishna*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了SCALE-Sim TPU，一个经过验证和扩展的TPU风格加速器周期精确模拟器，通过硬件验证、轻量级延迟模型和现代ML编译器集成，显著提升了模拟的准确性、覆盖范围和实用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的脉动加速器周期精确模拟器存在两个主要局限性：1）与真实硬件的验证较弱，导致准确性不足；2）与现代机器学习编译器栈的集成较差，限制了其实用性。这些缺陷阻碍了全模型性能分析的可靠性。

Method: 1）将SCALE-Sim的脉动GEMM模型与Google TPU v4硬件测量结果进行验证，建立周期到延迟的线性映射关系；2）针对非脉动逐元素操作，引入基于张量大小和形状的轻量级学习延迟模型；3）开发基于StableHLO的前端，支持JAX和PyTorch等现代ML框架的工作负载直接通过统一编译器中间表示进行模拟。

Result: 1）模拟周期数与TPU v4硬件延迟呈现强线性相关性，验证了模型的准确性；2）轻量级延迟模型在非脉动操作上实现了中位相对误差低于3%的精度；3）成功实现了现代ML框架工作负载的直接模拟功能。

Conclusion: SCALE-Sim TPU通过硬件验证、改进的非脉动操作建模和现代编译器集成，在保真度、覆盖范围和实用性三个维度上显著提升了TPU全模型性能分析的能力，为脉动加速器研究提供了更可靠的周期精确模拟工具。

Abstract: Cycle-accurate simulators are widely used to study systolic accelerators, yet their accuracy and usability are often limited by weak validation against real hardware and poor integration with modern ML compiler stacks. This paper presents SCALE-Sim TPU, a validated and extended version of SCALE-Sim v3 for TPU-style accelerators. Specifically, we make three contributions: (1) We validate SCALE-Sim's systolic GEMM model against measurements on Google TPU v4 and show that simulated cycle counts exhibit a strong linear correlation with hardware latency, enabling a simple cycle-to-latency mapping. (2) We introduce lightweight learned latency models for non-systolic elementwise operations, achieving median relative errors below 3 percent using only tensor size and shape, substantially improving end-to-end latency estimation. (3) We integrate a StableHLO-based frontend that allows workloads from modern ML frameworks such as JAX and PyTorch to be simulated directly via a unified compiler IR. Together, these contributions improve the fidelity, coverage, and practicality of cycle-accurate simulation for whole-model performance analysis on TPUs.

</details>


### [2] [CPU Simulation with Ranked Set Sampling and Repeated Subsampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22598)
*Magnus Ekman*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出将秩集采样（RSS）应用于计算机架构模拟，相比简单随机采样可将置信区间宽度减少高达50%；并引入重复子采样方案选择代表性模拟区域，将最大误差从35%降至10%，在SPEC CPU 2017整数应用上实现了低于2%的平均误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 计算机系统模拟研究通常需要执行有限数量的短应用区域，因为完整的端到端模拟非常耗时。现有方法采用随机采样或基于阶段的特征化来识别代表性区域，但需要更好的采样方法来提高模拟的代表性和准确性。

Method: 1) 重新审视计算机架构模拟中的随机采样，研究样本标准差与样本均值的关系以评估置信水平；2) 将统计学中成熟的秩集采样（RSS）技术应用于架构模拟；3) 引入重复子采样方案来识别代表性模拟区域；4) 评估两种选择标准以进一步提高准确性。

Result: RSS相比简单随机采样将置信区间宽度减少了高达50%；重复子采样方案在固定样本大小下将最大观察误差从35%降低到10%；在SPEC CPU 2017整数应用上，模拟30个各100万条指令的区域时，实现了低于2%的平均误差和3.5%的最大误差。

Conclusion: 秩集采样技术自然地映射到架构模拟场景，并能提供比简单随机采样更紧的置信区间。结合重复子采样方案和更明智的子样本选择，可以显著提高模拟的准确性和可靠性，为未来的模拟研究提供有效的代表性区域选择方法。

Abstract: Computer system simulation studies routinely rely on executing a limited number of short application regions, since full end-to-end simulation is prohibitively time-consuming. To preserve representativeness, existing methods employ either random sampling or phase-based characterization to identify representative regions.
  In this work, we revisit random sampling in the context of computer architecture simulation. To assess how the confidence level varies with different micro-architectural configurations, we examine how the sample standard deviation relates to the sample mean. We show that the ranked set sampling (RSS) technique - well established in the statistical literature - maps naturally to architectural simulation and yields significantly tighter confidence intervals than simple random sampling. Across our experiments, RSS reduces the confidence interval width by up to 50%.
  We further introduce a repeated subsampling scheme that identifies representative simulation regions for future studies. For a fixed sample size, this approach reduces the maximum observed error from 35% to 10%. Evaluating two selection criteria, we find that more informed subsample selection provides additional accuracy gains. Overall, our method achieves an average error below 2% and a maximum error of 3.5% across individual SPEC CPU 2017 Integer applications when simulating 30 regions of 1 million instructions each.

</details>


### [3] [CPU Simulation Using Two-Phase Stratified Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22605)
*Magnus Ekman*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文重新审视了SimPoint在SPEC CPU 2017基准测试中的表现，发现传统SimPoint方法存在显著的性能预测误差（部分应用误差高达40-60%），并提出了一种两阶段分层抽样方法，将最大单应用误差降至3%，同时实现了比简单随机抽样低一个数量级的样本量需求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 计算机架构研究严重依赖模拟器，但完整的应用程序执行极其耗时。虽然业界标准的SimPoint方法通过选择代表性代码区域来缓解这一问题，但作者质疑在SPEC CPU 2017 Integer套件中，程序阶段行为的显著性是否足够，以及典型SimPoint使用方式能获得什么样的精度。已有数据表明，常见的SimPoint数量会导致显著的估计误差，这促使作者深入研究该问题并寻求改进方案。

Method: 首先将SimPoint重新构建为分层抽样问题，从而推导出保守的置信区间。随后将SimPoint分解为两个基本组成部分：分层（聚类）和样本单元选择（质心选择），并分析它们对精度的各自影响。在此基础上，提出一种两阶段（双重）抽样方案：第一阶段进行大规模的初步随机抽样以实现改进的分层和更具代表性的区域选择，第二阶段进行精准的采样分析。

Result: 实证分析证实，使用20个SimPoint时，两个应用程序表现出40-60%的性能预测误差。而采用所提出的两阶段分层框架后，最大单应用误差降至3%。此外，该方法在保持严格分析置信区间的同时，相比简单随机抽样实现了所需样本量减少一个数量级的效果。

Conclusion: 本文揭示了传统SimPoint方法在性能预测精度方面的严重不足，并通过统计抽样理论提出了可靠的解决方案。提出的两阶段分层框架为统计上有据可依且高效的架构模拟提供了实用路径，该方法不仅显著提高了预测精度，还大幅降低了模拟成本，具有重要的实践意义和推广价值。

Abstract: Simulation remains a cornerstone of computer architecture research, yet full end-to-end application execution is prohibitively time-consuming. The industry-standard solution, SimPoint, mitigates this cost by selecting a small number of representative code regions that capture program phase behavior. In this work, we take a fresh look at phase behavior in the SPEC CPU 2017 Integer suite to assess how pronounced such behavior truly is and what accuracy can be expected from typical SimPoint usage. Based on previously published data, we argue that common SimPoint counts can induce substantial estimation errors. To explore this further, we recast SimPoint as a stratified sampling problem, which enables the derivation of a conservative confidence interval. The analysis indicates that significant errors are expected, and our empirical analysis confirms this: with 20 SimPoints, two applications exhibit 40-60% performance prediction error.
  We decompose SimPoint into its two fundamental components - stratification (clustering) and sample-unit selection (centroid choice) - and analyze their individual effects on accuracy. We then extend the approach into a two-phase (double) sampling scheme, in which a large preliminary random sample enables improved stratification and more representative region selection. Using this method, the maximum per-application error drops to 3%. Finally, we demonstrate that the proposed two-phase stratified framework achieves an order-of-magnitude reduction in required sample size compared to simple random sampling while maintaining a tight analytical confidence interval, suggesting a practical path toward statistically grounded and efficient architectural simulation.

</details>


### [4] [Characterizing CPU-Induced Slowdowns in Multi-GPU LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22774)
*Euijun Chung,Yuxiao Jia,Aaron Jezghani,Hyesoon Kim*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Large-scale machine learning workloads increasingly rely on multi-GPU systems, yet their performance is often limited by an overlooked component: the CPU. Through a detailed study of modern large language model (LLM) inference and serving workloads, we find that multi-GPU performance frequently degrades not because GPUs are saturated, but because CPUs fail to keep the GPUs busy. Under limited CPU allocations, systems exhibit symptoms such as delayed kernel launch, stalled communication, and increased tokenization latency, leading to severe GPU underutilization even when ample GPU resources are available. This work presents a systematic analysis of CPU-induced slowdowns in multi-GPU LLM inference. We show that these bottlenecks persist even in serving stacks that employ process-level separation and modern GPU-side optimizations such as CUDA Graphs. Since the marginal cost of additional CPU cores is small relative to GPU instance pricing, our evaluation indicates that increasing the number of CPU cores can substantially improve performance and stability at minimal additional cost. Under moderate serving load, we observe that CPU-starved configurations frequently time out, while providing adequate CPU resources restores responsiveness and reduces time-to-first-token (TTFT) latency by 1.36-5.40x across configurations, all without requiring additional GPUs. This work shows that CPU provisioning is a crucial factor in multi-GPU LLM inference configuration, helping prevent control-side bottlenecks.

</details>


### [5] [TorR: Towards Brain-Inspired Task-Oriented Reasoning via Cache-Oriented Algorithm-Architecture Co-design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22855)
*Hyunwoo Oh,SungHeon Jeong,Suyeon Jang,Hanning Chen,Sanggeon Yun,Tamoghno Das,Mohsen Imani*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: TorR提出了一种脑启发的算法-架构协同设计，用超维关联推理替代CLIP风格的密集对齐，在保持检测精度的同时实现了数量级的能耗降低，实现了边缘设备上的实时任务导向目标检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于CLIP的任务导向目标检测虽然提供了开放词汇表和提示驱动的语义能力，但其密集的逐窗口计算和大量内存流量阻碍了在实时、功率受限的边缘设备上的部署，亟需一种能够降低计算开销和能耗的解决方案。

Method: 提出TorR框架，采用算法-架构协同设计方法。算法层面：将对齐问题重构为超维计算相似性和图组合，引入部分相似性重用机制，包括查询缓存与每类分数累积、仅少量超向量位变化时的精确δ更新、高系统负载时的相似性/负载门控旁路。架构层面：实现通道可扩展的位切片项目存储器，配备银行/精度门控和轻量级控制器，根据目标数量动态调度旁路/δ/完整路径以满足RT-30/RT-60目标。

Result: 在TSMC 28nm工艺下综合实现，通过周期精确仿真验证：在60 FPS下能耗约50mJ/窗口，30 FPS下约113mJ/窗口，延迟抖动低；在五个任务提示上AP@0.5均值达44.27%，与强VLM基线保持有界差距，但能耗降低数个数量级；支持部署时配置（有效维度D'、阈值、精度）以权衡精度、延迟和能耗。

Conclusion: TorR成功展示了通过脑启发的算法-架构协同设计，用超维关联推理替代传统密集对齐方法，能够在边缘设备预算约束下实现实时、高效的任务导向目标检测，为开放词汇表目标检测的边缘部署提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Task-oriented object detection (TOOD) atop CLIP offers open-vocabulary, prompt-driven semantics, yet dense per-window computation and heavy memory traffic hinder real-time, power-limited edge deployment. We present \emph{TorR}, a brain-inspired \textbf{algorithm--architecture co-design} that \textbf{replaces CLIP-style dense alignment with a hyperdimensional (HDC) associative reasoner} and turns temporal coherence into reuse. On the \emph{algorithm} side, TorR reformulates alignment as HDC similarity and graph composition, introducing \emph{partial-similarity reuse} via (i) query caching with per-class score accumulation, (ii) exact $δ$-updates when only a small set of hypervector bits change, and (iii) similarity/load-gated bypass under high system load. On the \emph{architecture} side, TorR instantiates a lane-scalable, bit-sliced item memory with bank/precision gating and a lightweight controller that schedules bypass/$δ$/full paths to meet RT-30/RT-60 targets as object counts vary. Synthesized in a TSMC 28\,nm process and exercised with a cycle-accurate simulator, TorR sustains real-time throughput with millijoule-scale energy per window ($\approx$50\,mJ at 60\,FPS; $\approx$113\,mJ at 30\,FPS) and low latency jitter, while delivering competitive AP@0.5 across five task prompts (mean 44.27\%) within a bounded margin to strong VLM baselines, but at orders-of-magnitude lower energy. The design exposes deployment-time configurability (effective dimension $D'$, thresholds, precision) to trade accuracy, latency, and energy for edge budgets.

</details>


### [6] [TRINE: A Token-Aware, Runtime-Adaptive FPGA Inference Engine for Multimodal AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22867)
*Hyunwoo Oh,Hanning Chen,Sanggeon Yun,Yang Ni,Suyeon Jang,Behnam Khaleghi,Fei Wen,Mohsen Imani*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: TRINE是一种单比特流FPGA加速器和编译器，通过统一的数据流模式映射和运行时模式切换，在20-21W功耗下实现了比RTX 4090快22.57倍的低延迟端到端多模态推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合ViT、CNN、GNN和Transformer NLP的多模态模型在嵌入式平台上面临严峻挑战：不同计算/内存模式差异巨大，且硬实时目标几乎不留余量，导致传统方案难以满足性能需求。

Method: 提出TRINE单比特流FPGA加速器，将各层统一为DDMM/SDDMM/SpMM算子，映射到可在权值驻留脉动阵列、1xCS SIMD和可路由加法树(RADT)三种模式间运行时切换的可重构PE阵列；采用宽度匹配的两阶段top-k单元实现流内token剪枝，以及依赖感知层卸载(DALO)技术在可重构处理单元间重叠独立核函数以维持高利用率。

Result: 在Alveo U50和ZCU104上评测，TRINE在20-21W功耗下比RTX 4090降低延迟达22.57倍，比Jetson Orin Nano快6.86倍；单独token剪枝在ViT密集流水线上带来高达7.8倍提升，DALO贡献高达79%吞吐量改善；int8量化下准确率损失<2.5%。

Conclusion: TRINE成功实现了统一视觉、语言和图工作负载的单比特流端到端多模态推理，在保持精度的同时达到了最先进的延迟和能效，为嵌入式平台的多模态部署提供了高效解决方案。

Abstract: Multimodal stacks that mix ViTs, CNNs, GNNs, and transformer NLP strain embedded platforms because their compute/memory patterns diverge and hard real-time targets leave little slack. TRINE is a single-bitstream FPGA accelerator and compiler that executes end-to-end multimodal inference without reconfiguration. Layers are unified as DDMM/SDDMM/SpMM and mapped to a mode-switchable engine that toggles at runtime among weight/output-stationary systolic, 1xCS SIMD, and a routable adder tree (RADT) on a shared PE array. A width-matched, two-stage top-k unit enables in-stream token pruning, while dependency-aware layer offloading (DALO) overlaps independent kernels across reconfigurable processing units to sustain utilization. Evaluated on Alveo U50 and ZCU104, TRINE reduces latency by up to 22.57x vs. RTX 4090 and 6.86x vs. Jetson Orin Nano at 20-21 W; token pruning alone yields up to 7.8x on ViT-heavy pipelines, and DALO contributes up to 79% throughput improvement. With int8 quantization, accuracy drops remain <2.5% across representative tasks, delivering state-of-the-art latency and energy efficiency for unified vision, language, and graph workloads-in one bitstream.

</details>

### [7] [Linux and High-Performance Computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22495)
*David A. Bader*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文分析了1990年代Linux高性能计算（HPC）领域两种主要的并行计算方法：Beowulf集群架构与Roadrunner架构的技术决策、性能影响及其长期影响力，旨在评估二者对基于Linux超级计算机发展的贡献。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 20世纪80年代HPC成本高昂（如Cray-2价格高达1200-1700万美元），主要局限于政府实验室和国家超算中心。90年代随着科学应用数据量激增和计算复杂度提高，研究人员开始探索使用Linux服务器集群构建低成本HPC系统。Beowulf和Roadrunner作为两种不同的技术路线应运而生，需要系统分析其技术选择与长期影响。

Method: 对Beowulf和Roadrunner两种Linux并行计算架构进行技术历史分析：Beowulf作为低成本个人计算机集群方法，服务于NASA个体研究者；Roadrunner整合商用组件与专用网络技术，构建可与商用超级计算机竞争的多用户系统。通过对比分析两种方法的设计理念、技术实现路径及目标用户群体的差异，评估其性能特征。

Result: 识别了两种架构的核心差异：Beowulf以极低成本和可访问性为优势，面向个体研究者的并行工作站需求；Roadrunner则在保持相对低成本的同时集成专用网络技术，定位为可与传统商用超级计算机竞争的多用户系统。分析揭示了不同技术路线的性能影响与适用场景。

Conclusion: 通过对Beowulf和Roadrunner技术决策、性能表现及历史影响的分析，为评估这两种方法对基于Linux超级计算机发展的长期贡献提供了基础框架，有助于理解不同设计哲学对HPC领域发展的影响。

Abstract: In the 1980s, high-performance computing (HPC) became another tool for research in the open (non-defense) science and engineering research communities. However, HPC came with a high price tag; the first Cray-2 machines, released in 1985, cost between \$12 million and \$17 million, according to the Computer History Museum, and were largely available only at government research labs or through national supercomputing centers. In the 1990s, with demand for HPC increasing due to vast datasets, more complex modeling, and the growing computational needs of scientific applications, researchers began experimenting with building HPC machines from clusters of servers running the Linux operating system. By the late 1990s, two approaches to Linux-based parallel computing had emerged: the personal computer cluster methodology that became known as Beowulf and the Roadrunner architecture aimed at a more cost-effective supercomputer. While Beowulf attracted attention because of its low cost and thereby greater accessibility, Roadrunner took a different approach. While still affordable compared to vector processors and other commercially available supercomputers, Roadrunner integrated its commodity components with specialized networking technology. Furthermore, these systems initially served different purposes. While Beowulf focused on providing affordable parallel workstations for individual researchers at NASA, Roadrunner set out to provide a multi-user system that could compete with the commercial supercomputers that dominated the market at the time. This paper analyzes the technical decisions, performance implications, and long-term influence of both approaches. Through this analysis, we can start to judge the impact of both Roadrunner and Beowulf on the development of Linux-based supercomputers.

</details>


### [8] [Interactive and Urgent HPC: State of the Research](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22542)
*Albert Reuther,William Arndt,Johannes Blaschke,Christian Boehme,Nick Brown,Antony Chazapis,Bjoern Enders,Jens Henrik Goebbert,Robert Henschel,Julian Kunkel,Maxime Martinasso,Michael Ringenburg,Rollin Thomas*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文综述了交互式和紧迫性高性能计算（HPC）的发展现状与未来路径，旨在推动HPC生态系统中相关策略、技术和方法的深度融合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着智能手机、电子商务、协作平台和大语言模型（LLM）等日常计算应用日益呈现交互性和紧迫性特征，相似趋势正进入HPC领域。模拟、数据分析和机器学习等应用对并行计算能力及交互性提出更高要求，因此需要在HPC生态中引入并整合交互式与紧迫性策略与技术。

Method: 本文采用综述性方法，概述了交互式和紧迫性HPC的已有进展，并提出了未来发展的方向与路径。

Result: 总结了当前交互式和紧迫性HPC的主要技术与政策进展，并识别了将其更深度集成到HPC生态系统中的关键方向。

Conclusion: 未来HPC生态系统需要更紧密地融合交互式和紧迫性策略、技术与方法，以满足模拟、数据分析与机器学习等新兴应用日益增长的并行计算与交互需求。

Abstract: When we think of how we use smartphones, e-commerce, collaboration platforms, LLMs, etc., most of our interactions with computers are interactive and often urgent. Similar trends of interactivity and urgency are coming to HPC, with applications from simulations to data analysis and machine learning requiring more parallel computational capability and more interactivity. This chapter overviews the progress made so far along with some vectors of what the path forward will bring for greater integration of interactive and urgent HPC policies, techniques, and technologies into our HPC ecosystems.

</details>


### [9] [Rank-Aware Resource Scheduling for Tightly-Coupled MPI Workloads on Kubernetes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22691)
*Tianfang Xie*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于Kubernetes的MPI等级感知资源调度方法，通过按子区域网格数量成比例配置CPU资源，在保持CFD求解器性能的同时显著提高了集群资源利用效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的完全配置MPI并行化虽然能实现接近最优的运行时间，但在共享云管理集群中，低负载子域存在资源预留过度的问题。研究旨在探索细粒度CPU配置能否减少低负载子域的资源预留，在不严重降低性能的前提下提升集群打包效率。

Method: 提出Kubernetes上的等级感知资源调度策略，将每个MPI等级映射到一个Pod，其CPU请求量与子区域网格数量成正比。同时演示了Kubernetes v1.35 GA版本的In-Place Pod垂直扩缩容功能，实现在模拟过程中无需重启Pod即可调整CPU资源。

Result: 三个核心发现：（1）通过Linux CFS带宽控制器设置硬CPU限制会导致MPI_Allreduce屏障处出现级联停滞，造成78倍减速；而仅使用请求分配可完全消除节流。（2）在非突发型c5.xlarge实例上，同心分解配合等量CPU比Scotch基线快19%，添加按比例CPU配置额外提升3%。（3）在101K网格、16个MPI等级下，按比例分配比等量分配快20%，同时将稀疏子域配置CPU减少82%，释放6.5 vCPU调度余量。

Conclusion: 细粒度的等级感知CPU配置在CFD求解器场景中具有可行性，能够在保持甚至提升性能的同时大幅提高集群资源利用效率，为云环境下的HPC工作负载调度提供了新思路。

Abstract: Fully provisioned Message Passing Interface (MPI) parallelism achieves near-optimal wall-clock time for Computational Fluid Dynamics (CFD) solvers. This work addresses a complementary question for shared, cloud-managed clusters: can fine-grained CPU provisioning reduce resource reservation of low-load subdomains, improving cluster packing efficiency without unacceptably degrading performance?
  We propose rank-aware resource scheduling on Kubernetes, mapping each MPI rank to a pod whose CPU request is proportional to its subdomain cell count. We also demonstrate In-Place Pod Vertical Scaling (Kubernetes v1.35 GA) for mid-simulation CPU adjustment without pod restart.
  Three findings emerge. First, hard CPU limits via the Linux CFS bandwidth controller cause 78x slowdown through cascading stalls at MPI_Allreduce barriers; requests-only allocation eliminates throttling entirely. Second, on non-burstable c5.xlarge instances, concentric decomposition with equal CPU is 19% faster than the Scotch baseline, while adding proportional CPU yields a further 3% improvement. Third, at 16 MPI ranks on 101K-cell meshes, proportional allocation is 20% faster than equal allocation while reducing sparse-subdomain provisioned CPU by 82%, freeing 6.5 vCPU of scheduling headroom.
  Experiments are conducted on AWS EC2 c5.xlarge clusters (4-16 ranks) running k3s v1.35. All scripts and data are released as open source.

</details>


### [10] [PCR: A Prefetch-Enhanced Cache Reuse System for Low-Latency RAG Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23049)
*Wenfeng Wang,Xiaofeng Hou,Peng Tang,Hengyi Zhou,Jing Wang,Xinkai Wang,Chao Li,Minyi Guo*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出PCR系统，通过智能预取和流水线数据移动最大化KV缓存重用效率，在平均TTFT上实现高达2.47倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检索增强生成(RAG)系统中，整合外部文档导致输入序列过长，显著增加了预填充阶段的计算成本。现有KV缓存重用方法受限于缓存命中率低、CPU-GPU数据传输开销大以及SSD I/O速度慢这三个实际问题，限制了缓存重用的有效性。

Method: PCR系统引入三项关键技术：(1) 基于前缀树的缓存结构，配合前瞻LRU替换策略，利用调度队列中的待处理请求提高缓存命中率；(2) 层级重叠技术，通过CUDA流将KV缓存加载与GPU计算流水线化，隐藏通信延迟；(3) 基于队列的预取机制，在需要前主动将相关KV缓存从SSD加载到DRAM。

Result: 大量实验表明，PCR优于现有KV缓存重用方法，在平均TTFT(首个token时间)上实现了高达2.47倍的加速。

Conclusion: PCR系统通过智能预取、流水线数据移动和改进的缓存管理策略，有效解决了RAG系统中KV缓存重用的三大挑战，显著降低了预填充阶段的延迟，为高吞吐量服务场景下的RAG系统提供了有效的优化方案。

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) systems enhance the performance of large language models (LLMs) by incorporating supplementary retrieved documents, enabling more accurate and context-aware responses. However, integrating these external documents often results in very long input sequences, which significantly increases computation costs during the prefill stage, where key-value (KV) representations for all input tokens are generated. This latency bottleneck becomes especially pronounced under high-throughput serving scenarios. KV-cache reuse offers a promising solution by storing previously computed KV states for shared input prefixes, thereby avoiding redundant computation across requests that contain overlapping context. Yet, the effectiveness of cache reuse is often limited by three practical challenges: low cache hit rates due to naive eviction policies, high CPU-GPU data transfer overhead, and slow SSD I/O when caches spill to storage. To address these issues, we propose PCR, a system designed to maximize KV-cache reuse efficiency through intelligent prefetching and pipelined data movement. Specifically, PCR introduces three key techniques: (1) a prefix-tree caching structure with a look-ahead LRU replacement policy that uses pending requests in the scheduler queue to improve cache hit ratios; (2) layer-wise overlapping that pipelines KV-cache loading and GPU computation across CUDA streams to hide communication latency; and (3) queue-based prefetching that proactively loads relevant KV caches from SSD into DRAM before they are needed. Extensive experiments show that PCR outperforms existing KV-cache reuse methods, achieving up to a 2.47x speedup in terms of average TTFT.

</details>


### [11] [Communication-Aware Diffusion Load Balancing for Persistently Interacting Objects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23329)
*Maya Taylor,Kavitha Chandrasekar,Laxmikant V. Kale*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向通信密集型应用的分布式扩散式负载均衡策略，利用通信图减少跨节点通信，并为未知通信模式提供了算法变体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 并行应用中不规则且时变的工作负载常导致负载不均衡，而现有的动态负载均衡技术在通信密集型应用中可能引入过多通信开销。

Method: 提出一种利用应用通信图的分布式扩散式负载均衡方法，以在有效分发负载的同时减少跨节点通信；并针对通信模式未知的情况提出了算法变体。

Result: 通过模拟实验以及在NERSC Perlmutter超算上8节点的Particle-in-Cell基准测试，验证了该方法相较于相关策略的有效性，并探讨了算法优化。

Conclusion: 该方法能够在通信密集型并行应用中实现高效的动态负载均衡，在降低跨节点通信开销的同时保持良好的负载分布效果。

Abstract: Parallel applications with irregular and time-varying workloads often suffer from load imbalance. Dynamic load balancing techniques address this challenge by redistributing work during execution. We present a new type of distributed diffusion-based load balancing targeted at communication-intensive applications with persistently communicating objects. Leveraging the application's communication graph, our strategy reduces across-node communication while simultaneously distributing load effectively. We also propose an algorithmic variant for cases where the communication patterns are not readily available. We explore optimizations to our algorithm, and comparisons with other related load balancing strategies in simulation and on a Particle-in-Cell benchmark on up to 8 nodes of Perlmutter at NERSC.

</details>

### [12] [Beyond Hard Constraints: Budget-Conditioned Reachability For Safe Offline Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22292)
*Janaka Chathuranga Brahmanage,Akshat Kumar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于安全性条件可达性集合的离线安全强化学习算法，在无需环境交互的情况下，成功解耦了奖励最大化与累积安全成本约束，在基准测试和实际任务中均达到最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实世界的序列决策任务需要在奖励最大化与安全约束之间取得平衡。现有的基于拉格朗日或对抗优化的方法存在不稳定的min/max优化问题。虽然安全性可达性分析可提供前向不变的安全状态-动作集合，但现有工作多局限于硬安全约束，缺乏对累积成本约束的扩展，亟需一种稳定且适用于离线场景的新方法。

Method: 首先，定义了“安全性条件可达性集合”（safety-conditioned reachability set），将奖励最大化与累积安全成本约束进行解耦；其次，基于该集合提出了一种新型离线安全强化学习算法，该算法无需不稳定的min/max或拉格朗日优化，可直接从固定数据集中学习安全策略，且不需要与环境交互。

Result: 在标准离线安全强化学习基准测试以及真实世界的海上导航任务上的实验表明，该方法在保持安全性的同时，性能达到或超越了现有的最先进基线算法。

Conclusion: 本文提出的安全性条件可达性集合为离线安全强化学习提供了一种有效的新范式，成功避免了传统方法中的不稳定优化问题，在理论和实验上均验证了其在安全约束下的优越性能，具有重要的实际应用价值。

Abstract: Sequential decision making using Markov Decision Process underpins many realworld applications. Both model-based and model free methods have achieved strong results in these settings. However, real-world tasks must balance reward maximization with safety constraints, often conflicting objectives, that can lead to unstable min/max, adversarial optimization. A promising alternative is safety reachability analysis, which precomputes a forward-invariant safe state, action set, ensuring that an agent starting inside this set remains safe indefinitely. Yet, most reachability based methods address only hard safety constraints, and little work extends reachability to cumulative cost constraints. To address this, first, we define a safetyconditioned reachability set that decouples reward maximization from cumulative safety cost constraints. Second, we show how this set enforces safety constraints without unstable min/max or Lagrangian optimization, yielding a novel offline safe RL algorithm that learns a safe policy from a fixed dataset without environment interaction. Finally, experiments on standard offline safe RL benchmarks, and a real world maritime navigation task demonstrate that our method matches or outperforms state of the art baselines while maintaining safety.

</details>


### [13] [Efficient Embedding-based Synthetic Data Generation for Complex Reasoning Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22294)
*Srideepika Jayaraman,Achille Fokoue,Dhaval Patel,Jayant Kalagnanam*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于嵌入空间的定向采样方法，通过提升合成数据的多样性来增强小型LLM的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前利用大语言模型生成合成数据(SDG)来微调小型模型时，如何确保生成数据的质量与多样性是关键挑战。作者发现嵌入空间中特定邻域内的样本密度与该区域预测准确率之间存在强相关性，需要解决数据分布不均的问题。

Method: 作者首先分析生成数据在嵌入空间中的多样性与分布，验证样本密度与预测准确率的关联；基于此洞察，提出了一种基于嵌入空间的定向采样管线，以增强数据的多样性。

Result: 实验证明，所提方法在多个基准测试上均取得一致的性能提升，验证了增强数据多样性对模型效果的积极作用。

Conclusion: 通过在嵌入空间中有针对性地采样以提升数据多样性，可以有效提高合成数据微调小型模型的性能表现，为SDG提供了一条可行的优化路径。

Abstract: Synthetic Data Generation (SDG), leveraging Large Language Models (LLMs), has recently been recognized and broadly adopted as an effective approach to improve the performance of smaller but more resource and compute efficient LLMs through fine-tuning. A key challenge in SDG is ensuring the quality and diversity of the generated data. In this paper, we analyze the diversity and distribution of generated data in the embedding space, and demonstrate a strong correlation between the density of examples within a specific neighborhood and the accuracy of predictions on examples drawn from that region. Building on this insight, we present a targeted pipeline for embedding-based sampling that enhances data diversity and consistently improves performance across several benchmarks.

</details>


### [14] [Behavioral Heterogeneity as Quantum-Inspired Representation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22729)
*Mohammad Elayan,Wissam Kontar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种量子启发的驾驶员表示方法，将驾驶员建模为演化的潜在状态（密度矩阵），在TGSIM数据上验证了驾驶行为特征的提取与分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的驾驶员异质性研究常将其简化为静态标签或离散类别，忽略了驾驶行为本质上的动态特性，因此需要一种能够捕捉驾驶员行为动态演化的表示方法。

Method: 提出量子启发的表示方法，将驾驶员建模为具有结构化数学性质的密度矩阵形式的演化潜在状态；利用非线性随机傅里叶特征嵌入行为观测数据，状态演化过程融合了行为的时间持续性与情境依赖的驾驶特征激活。

Result: 在经验驾驶数据（第三代仿真数据，TGSIM）上对所提方法进行了评估，展示了驾驶特征（profiles）的提取与分析过程。

Conclusion: 该方法能有效捕捉驾驶员的动态异质性，为驾驶行为的动态建模提供了新的理论框架与工具。

Abstract: Driver heterogeneity is often reduced to labels or discrete regimes, compressing what is inherently dynamic into static categories. We introduce quantum-inspired representation that models each driver as an evolving latent state, presented as a density matrix with structured mathematical properties. Behavioral observations are embedded via non-linear Random Fourier Features, while state evolution blends temporal persistence of behavior with context-dependent profile activation. We evaluate our approach on empirical driving data, Third Generation Simulation Data (TGSIM), showing how driving profiles are extracted and analyzed.

</details>


### [15] [A Theoretical Framework for Energy-Aware Gradient Pruning in Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22465)
*Emmanouil M. Athanasakos*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出成本加权幅度剪枝（CWMP）方法，通过考虑参数更新的物理成本差异，在联邦学习中实现了优于Top-K基准的性能-能耗帕累托前沿。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习受限于边缘设备的通信和能耗约束，现有的Top-K幅度剪枝虽然减少了通信负载，但忽略了硬件层面内存密集型和计算高效型操作之间的成本差异，假设所有参数更新具有相同的传输和内存更新成本，这与实际硬件情况不符。

Method: 将剪枝过程形式化为能量约束投影问题，提出成本加权幅度剪枝（CWMP）选择规则，该规则根据参数更新的幅度与其物理成本的相对关系来确定优先级，证明了CWMP是该约束投影问题的最优贪心解，并对其全局能效进行了概率分析。

Result: 在非独立同分布CIFAR-10基准测试上的数值结果表明，CWMP始终建立起优于Top-K基准的性能-能耗帕累托前沿，实现了更好的性能与能耗权衡。

Conclusion: CWMP通过考虑硬件层面的操作成本差异，有效地解决了传统梯度稀疏化方法的能量盲目性问题，为联邦学习中的能耗优化提供了一种理论完备且实际有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Federated Learning (FL) is constrained by the communication and energy limitations of decentralized edge devices. While gradient sparsification via Top-K magnitude pruning effectively reduces the communication payload, it remains inherently energy-agnostic. It assumes all parameter updates incur identical downstream transmission and memory-update costs, ignoring hardware realities. We formalize the pruning process as an energy-constrained projection problem that accounts for the hardware-level disparities between memory-intensive and compute-efficient operations during the post-backpropagation phase. We propose Cost-Weighted Magnitude Pruning (CWMP), a selection rule that prioritizes parameter updates based on their magnitude relative to their physical cost. We demonstrate that CWMP is the optimal greedy solution to this constrained projection and provide a probabilistic analysis of its global energy efficiency. Numerical results on a non-IID CIFAR-10 benchmark show that CWMP consistently establishes a superior performance-energy Pareto frontier compared to the Top-K baseline.

</details>


### [16] [Between the Layers Lies the Truth: Uncertainty Estimation in LLMs Using Intra-Layer Local Information Scores](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22299)
*Zvi N. Badash,Yonatan Belinkov,Moti Freiman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种紧凑、可迁移的不确定性估计方法，通过评分内部表示的跨层一致性模式，在单次前向传播中实现与探测方法相当的性能，且在跨数据集迁移和量化场景下表现更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型常出现“自信的错误”，需要可靠的不确定性估计。现有方法中，基于输出的启发式方法廉价但脆弱，而探测内部表示的方法虽然有效但维度高且难以迁移。因此需要一种既高效又可迁移的不确定性估计方法。

Method: 提出一种紧凑的、针对单个实例的不确定性估计方法。该方法利用单次前向传播，对内部表示中的跨层一致性模式进行评分，从而量化模型的不确定性。

Result: 在三个模型上的实验表明：（1）分布内性能与探测方法相当，AUPRC对角线差异最多为-1.8个百分点，Brier评分差异最多为+4.9个百分点；（2）跨数据集迁移时持续优于探测方法，非对角线增益最高达+2.86 AUPRC和+21.02 Brier评分点；（3）在4位仅权重量化下保持鲁棒性，平均提升+1.94 AUPRC和+5.33 Brier评分点。分析层间交互还揭示了不同模型编码不确定性的差异。

Conclusion: 该方法为捕获大语言模型中可迁移的不确定性提供了一种轻量、紧凑的手段，兼具高性能与良好的迁移性，且在量化场景下保持鲁棒性。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are often confidently wrong, making reliable uncertainty estimation (UE) essential. Output-based heuristics are cheap but brittle, while probing internal representations is effective yet high-dimensional and hard to transfer.
  We propose a compact, per-instance UE method that scores cross-layer agreement patterns in internal representations using a single forward pass.
  Across three models, our method matches probing in-distribution, with mean diagonal differences of at most $-1.8$ AUPRC percentage points and $+4.9$ Brier score points. Under cross-dataset transfer, it consistently outperforms probing, achieving off-diagonal gains up to $+2.86$ AUPRC and $+21.02$ Brier points. Under 4-bit weight-only quantization, it remains robust, improving over probing by $+1.94$ AUPRC points and $+5.33$ Brier points on average.
  Beyond performance, examining specific layer--layer interactions reveals differences in how disparate models encode uncertainty. Altogether, our UE method offers a lightweight, compact means to capture transferable uncertainty in LLMs.

</details>


### [17] [Scaling Attention via Feature Sparsity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22300)
*Yan Xie,Tiansheng Wen,Tangda Huang,Bo Chen,Chenyu You,Stefanie Jegelka,Yifei Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出稀疏特征注意力（SFA），通过在特征维度引入稀疏性将注意力复杂度从O(n²d)降至O(n²k²/d)，配合FlashSFA内核实现最高2.5倍加速、近50%的计算量和KV缓存减少，在几乎无损精度的前提下支持超长上下文。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法沿序列维度（如局部窗口、核近似、token级稀疏）降低自注意力O(n²d)成本，但会持续损害精度。作者希望探索一个正交方向——特征稀疏性，以在不牺牲高维表达能力的前提下高效扩展至超长上下文。

Method: 提出稀疏特征注意力（SFA），将查询和键表示为k稀疏编码；设计IO感知内核FlashSFA，扩展FlashAttention直接在稀疏重叠上操作，避免物化稠密得分矩阵。

Result: 在GPT-2和Qwen3预训练中匹配稠密基线，速度提升最高2.5倍，FLOPs与KV缓存降低近50%；在合成与下游基准上保持长上下文的检索准确性与鲁棒性，优于特征多样性崩塌的短嵌入基线。

Conclusion: 特征级稀疏性是高效注意力的互补且未被充分探索的轴，可支持将Transformer扩展至数量级更长的上下文且几乎不损失质量。

Abstract: Scaling Transformers to ultra-long contexts is bottlenecked by the $O(n^2 d)$ cost of self-attention. Existing methods reduce this cost along the sequence axis through local windows, kernel approximations, or token-level sparsity, but these approaches consistently degrade accuracy. In this paper, we instead explore an orthogonal axis: feature sparsity. We propose Sparse Feature Attention (SFA), where queries and keys are represented as $k$-sparse codes that preserve high-dimensional expressivity while reducing the cost of attention from $Θ(n^2 d)$ to $Θ(n^2 k^2/d)$. To make this efficient at scale, we introduce FlashSFA, an IO-aware kernel that extends FlashAttention to operate directly on sparse overlaps without materializing dense score matrices. Across GPT-2 and Qwen3 pretraining, SFA matches dense baselines while improving speed by up to $2.5\times$ and reducing FLOPs and KV-cache by nearly 50\%. On synthetic and downstream benchmarks, SFA preserves retrieval accuracy and robustness at long contexts, outperforming short-embedding baselines that collapse feature diversity. These results establish feature-level sparsity as a complementary and underexplored axis for efficient attention, enabling Transformers to scale to orders-of-magnitude longer contexts with minimal quality loss. Code is available at https://github.com/YannX1e/Sparse-Feature-Attention.

</details>


### [18] [Latent Semantic Manifolds in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22301)
*Mohamed A. Mabrok*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种将LLM隐藏状态解释为潜在语义流形的数学框架，揭示了词汇离散化带来的语义失真，并通过实验验证了普遍的几何规律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型（LLM）在连续向量空间中进行内部计算，却产生离散的token，这种根本性的不匹配所带来的几何后果尚未被充分理解。研究旨在建立数学框架来理解这种连续-离散失配的几何影响。

Method: 将LLM隐藏状态解释为配备Fisher信息度量的黎曼子流形上的点，其中token对应于划分流形的Voronoi区域。定义了"表达性间隙"作为词汇离散化造成的语义失真的几何度量，并证明了两个定理：关于有限词汇失真的率失真下界，以及通过余面积公式表达表达性间隙的线性体积缩放定律。

Result: 在六种Transformer架构（124M-1.5B参数）上验证了预测，确认了普遍的沙漏型内在维度轮廓、平滑曲率结构，以及斜率为0.87-1.12（R²>0.985）的线性间隙缩放。发现各模型的边界分布揭示了规模不变的边界邻近表示的持久硬核，提供了困惑度的几何分解。

Conclusion: 研究结果为架构设计、模型压缩、解码策略和缩放定律提供了重要启示，建立了理解LLM连续-离散失配问题的几何理论基础。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) perform internal computations in continuous vector spaces yet produce discrete tokens -- a fundamental mismatch whose geometric consequences remain poorly understood. We develop a mathematical framework that interprets LLM hidden states as points on a latent semantic manifold: a Riemannian submanifold equipped with the Fisher information metric, where tokens correspond to Voronoi regions partitioning the manifold. We define the expressibility gap, a geometric measure of the semantic distortion from vocabulary discretization, and prove two theorems: a rate-distortion lower bound on distortion for any finite vocabulary, and a linear volume scaling law for the expressibility gap via the coarea formula. We validate these predictions across six transformer architectures (124M-1.5B parameters), confirming universal hourglass intrinsic dimension profiles, smooth curvature structure, and linear gap scaling with slopes 0.87-1.12 (R^2 > 0.985). The margin distribution across models reveals a persistent hard core of boundary-proximal representations invariant to scale, providing a geometric decomposition of perplexity. We discuss implications for architecture design, model compression, decoding strategies, and scaling laws

</details>


### [19] [Research on Individual Trait Clustering and Development Pathway Adaptation Based on the K-means Algorithm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22302)
*Qianru Wei,Jihaoyu Yang,Cheng Zhang,Jinming Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究利用K-means聚类算法对3000多名大学生的CET-4成绩、GPA、性格特质和学生干部经历等数据进行分析，将学生划分为四类群体，并针对不同群体提供精准的职业指导建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究多集中于职业路径预测，较少关注不同特征组合的学生在特定职业方向上的匹配度。随着人工智能和机器学习在教育领域的发展，本研究旨在探索如何为学生提供更精准的职业指导。

Method: 收集3000多名学生的CET-4成绩、GPA、性格特质和学生干部经历等数据，采用K-means聚类算法，通过最小化类内平方误差将特征相似的学生归为同一组，确保同组内学生特征高度相似，不同组间差异最大化，最终将学生划分为四个主要群体。

Result: 不同特征组合的学生适合不同的职业方向；基于聚类结果提供的针对性职业指导建议，为个性化职业指导提供了科学依据，有效提升了学生的就业成功率。

Conclusion: K-means聚类算法能够有效识别具有不同特征组合的学生群体，为个性化职业指导提供支持。未来研究可通过扩大样本量、增加特征变量和考虑外部因素来进一步提升聚类精度和指导效果。

Abstract: With the development of information technology, the application of artificial intelligence and machine learning in the field of education shows great potential. This study aims to explore how to utilize K-means clustering algorithm to provide accurate career guidance for college students. Existing methods mostly focus on the prediction of career paths, but there are fewer studies on the fitness of students with different combinations of characteristics in specific career directions. In this study, we analyze the data of more than 3000 students on their CET-4 scores, GPA, personality traits and student cadre experiences, and use the K-means clustering algorithm to classify the students into four main groups. The K-means clustering algorithm groups students with similar characteristics into one group by minimizing the intra-cluster squared error, ensuring that the students within the same cluster are highly similar in their characteristics, and that differences between different clusters are maximized. Based on the clustering results, targeted career guidance suggestions are provided for each group. The results of the study show that students with different combinations of characteristics are suitable for different career directions, which provides a scientific basis for personalized career guidance and effectively enhances students' employment success rate. Future research can further improve the precision of clustering and the guidance effect by expanding the sample size, increasing the feature variables and considering external factors.

</details>


### [20] [Sample Transform Cost-Based Training-Free Hallucination Detector for Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22303)
*Zeyang Ding,Xinglin Hu,Jicong Fan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于最优传输理论的无训练幻觉检测方法，通过计算样本间Token嵌入的Wasserstein距离矩阵来量化大语言模型条件分布的复杂性，从而检测幻觉。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 幻觉问题是大语言模型可信部署的主要障碍，现有的检测方法需要一种准确、轻量且广泛适用的解决方案。由于大语言模型在给定提示下定义了一个条件分布，作者认为该分布的复杂性可以作为幻觉的指示器，但分布密度未知且样本为离散分布，给复杂性量化带来了挑战。

Method: 提出计算成对样本的Token嵌入集合之间的最优传输距离，生成Wasserstein距离矩阵来衡量样本间转换的成本。基于该矩阵推导出两个互补信号：AvgWD（测量平均成本）和EigenWD（测量成本复杂性），构建无训练的幻觉检测器。同时通过教师强制技术将框架扩展到黑盒大语言模型。

Result: 实验表明，AvgWD和EigenWD与强不确定性基线方法具有竞争力，在不同模型和数据集上展现出互补行为，验证了分布复杂性作为大语言模型真实性信号的有效性。

Conclusion: 基于Wasserstein距离的分布复杂性度量是一种有效的大语言模型幻觉检测信号，所提方法无需训练即可实现具有竞争力的检测性能，为构建可信的语言模型部署提供了新的技术路径。

Abstract: Hallucinations in large language models (LLMs) remain a central obstacle to trustworthy deployment, motivating detectors that are accurate, lightweight, and broadly applicable. Since an LLM with a prompt defines a conditional distribution, we argue that the complexity of the distribution is an indicator of hallucination. However, the density of the distribution is unknown and the samples (i.e., responses generated for the prompt) are discrete distributions, which leads to a significant challenge in quantifying the complexity of the distribution. We propose to compute the optimal-transport distances between the sets of token embeddings of pairwise samples, which yields a Wasserstein distance matrix measuring the costs of transforming between the samples. This Wasserstein distance matrix provides a means to quantify the complexity of the distribution defined by the LLM with the prompt. Based on the Wasserstein distance matrix, we derive two complementary signals: AvgWD, measuring the average cost, and EigenWD, measuring the cost complexity. This leads to a training-free detector for hallucinations in LLMs. We further extend the framework to black-box LLMs via teacher forcing with an accessible teacher model. Experiments show that AvgWD and EigenWD are competitive with strong uncertainty baselines and provide complementary behavior across models and datasets, highlighting distribution complexity as an effective signal for LLM truthfulness.

</details>


### [21] [Mitigating Premature Discretization with Progressive Quantization for Robust Vector Tokenization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22304)
*Wenhao Zhao,Qiran Zou,Zhouhan Lin,Dianbo Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了渐进式量化方法（ProVQ），通过将量化难度作为课程从连续潜空间平滑过渡到离散空间，解决了现有向量量化中“过早离散化”的根本冲突，在图像重建、生成任务和蛋白质结构标记化上均取得优异表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有向量量化范式存在一个根本性冲突：在编码器尚未捕捉到数据底层流形之前就强制执行离散化，这种现象被称为“过早离散化”，限制了模型性能。

Method: 提出渐进式量化（ProVQ），将量化硬度动态作为训练的基本轴，把量化过程视为一个课程，从连续潜空间平滑退火过渡到离散空间，从而引导码本向良好扩展的流形方向发展。

Result: 在ImageNet-1K和ImageNet-100基准上实现了重建和生成性能的提升；在复杂生物序列建模方面，在StrutTokenBench排行榜上为蛋白质结构标记化确立了新的性能上限。

Conclusion: ProVQ通过解决过早离散化问题，在多种模态下展现出广泛有效性，显著提升了生成建模能力和蛋白质结构标记化性能。

Abstract: Vector Quantization (VQ) has become the cornerstone of tokenization for many multimodal Large Language Models and diffusion synthesis. However, existing VQ paradigms suffer from a fundamental conflict: they enforce discretization before the encoder has captured the underlying data manifold. We term this phenomenon Premature Discretization. To resolve this, we propose Progressive Quantization (ProVQ), which incorporates the dynamics of quantization hardness as a fundamental yet previously overlooked axis in VQ training. By treating quantization as a curriculum that smoothly anneals from a continuous latent space to a discrete one, ProVQ effectively guides the codebook toward the well-expanded manifolds. Extensive experimental results demonstrate the broad effectiveness of ProVQ across diverse modalities. We report improved reconstruction and generative performance on the ImageNet-1K and ImageNet-100 benchmarks, highlighting the ProVQ's boost for generative modeling. Furthermore, ProVQ proves highly effective for modeling complex biological sequences, establishing a new performance ceiling for protein structure tokenization on the StrutTokenBench leaderboard.

</details>


### [22] [CN-Buzz2Portfolio: A Chinese-Market Dataset and Benchmark for LLM-Based Macro and Sector Asset Allocation from Daily Trending Financial News](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22305)
*Liyuan Chen,Shilong Li,Jiangpeng Yan,Shuoling Liu,Qiang Yang,Xiu Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了CN-Buzz2Portfolio基准，用于评估大语言模型在中国市场中将热门新闻转化为宏观和行业资产配置的能力，并设计了三阶段代理工作流程进行系统评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型正从静态NLP任务向动态金融决策代理转变，但现有评估范式面临两难困境：直接实盘交易不可复现且易受结果偏差影响，而现有静态基准仅限于个股选择，忽视了更广泛的市场关注。因此需要建立可复现、贴近现实的金融代理评估框架。

Method: 构建CN-Buzz2Portfolio基准数据集（涵盖2024年至2025年中期的滚动时间窗口），模拟真实的公众注意力流，要求代理从高曝光叙事中提炼投资逻辑。提出三阶段CPA代理工作流程（压缩Compressing-感知Perception-配置Allocation），在ETF等大类资产层面而非个股层面评估九个LLM模型将宏观叙事转化为投资组合权重的能力。

Result: 在九个大语言模型上的广泛实验揭示了模型将宏观叙事转化为投资组合权重的能力存在显著差异，展现了通用推理能力与金融决策之间的一致性程度。

Conclusion: 本研究为理解通用推理与金融决策之间的对齐关系提供了新见解，并公开所有数据、代码和实验结果以促进可持续的金融代理研究发展。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are rapidly transitioning from static Natural Language Processing (NLP) tasks including sentiment analysis and event extraction to acting as dynamic decision-making agents in complex financial environments. However, the evolution of LLMs into autonomous financial agents faces a significant dilemma in evaluation paradigms. Direct live trading is irreproducible and prone to outcome bias by confounding luck with skill, whereas existing static benchmarks are often confined to entity-level stock picking and ignore broader market attention. To facilitate the rigorous analysis of these challenges, we introduce CN-Buzz2Portfolio, a reproducible benchmark grounded in the Chinese market that maps daily trending news to macro and sector asset allocation. Spanning a rolling horizon from 2024 to mid-2025, our dataset simulates a realistic public attention stream, requiring agents to distill investment logic from high-exposure narratives instead of pre-filtered entity news. We propose a Tri-Stage CPA Agent Workflow involving Compression, Perception, and Allocation to evaluate LLMs on broad asset classes such as Exchange Traded Funds (ETFs) rather than individual stocks, thereby reducing idiosyncratic volatility. Extensive experiments on nine LLMs reveal significant disparities in how models translate macro-level narratives into portfolio weights. This work provides new insights into the alignment between general reasoning and financial decision-making, and all data, codes, and experiments are released to promote sustainable financial agent research.

</details>


### [23] [Full waveform inversion method based on diffusion model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22307)
*Caiyun Liu,Siyang Pei,Qingfeng Yu,Jie Xiong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于条件扩散模型正则化的地震全波形反演方法，通过将密度信息作为条件输入U-Net网络，利用速度与密度间的物理耦合关系进行约束，显著提升了反演结果的分辨率、结构保真度以及方法在复杂情况下的稳定性和鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 地震全波形反演具有高度非线性特征且对初始模型依赖性强，容易陷入局部极小值。现有基于生成扩散模型的正则化方法多采用无条件扩散过程，忽略了速度、密度等物理属性之间固有的物理耦合关系，未能充分利用这些先验信息来约束反演过程。

Method: 提出一种基于条件扩散模型正则化的全波形反演方法。该方法改进了扩散模型的主干网络结构，将二维密度信息作为条件输入引入到U-Net网络中，从而在扩散过程中显式地融入速度与密度之间的物理耦合关系，实现对反演过程的有效约束。

Result: 实验结果表明，所提出的方法显著提高了反演结果的分辨率和结构保真度；在处理复杂地质情况时，相比现有方法展现出更强的稳定性和鲁棒性，有效验证了利用密度信息约束反演的有效性。

Conclusion: 本文提出的条件扩散模型正则化全波形反演方法，通过引入密度条件信息有效利用了物理属性间的耦合关系，克服了传统方法易陷入局部极小值的缺陷，在提升反演质量的同时增强了方法的实用性，具有良好的实际应用价值。

Abstract: Seismic full-waveform inversion is a core technology for obtaining high-resolution subsurface model parameters. However, its highly nonlinear characteristics and strong dependence on the initial model often lead to the inversion process getting trapped in local minima. In recent years, generative diffusion models have provided a way to regularize full-waveform inversion by learning implicit prior distributions. However, existing methods mostly use unconditional diffusion processes, ignoring the inherent physical coupling relationship between velocity and density and other physical properties. This paper proposes a full-waveform inversion method based on conditional diffusion model regularization. By improving the backbone network structure of the diffusion model, two-dimensional density information is introduced as a conditional input into the U-Net network. Experimental results show that the full-waveform inversion method based on the conditional diffusion model significantly improves the resolution and structural fidelity of the inversion results, and exhibits stronger stability and robustness when dealing with complex situations. This method effectively utilizes density information to constrain the inversion and has good practical application value.
  Keywords: Deep learning; Diffusion model; Full waveform inversion.

</details>


### [24] [UniFluids: Unified Neural Operator Learning with Conditional Flow-matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22309)
*Haosen Li,Qi Meng,Jiahao Li,Rui Zhang,Ruihua Song,Liang Ma,Zhi-Ming Ma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了UniFluids，一个基于条件流匹配和扩散Transformer的统一框架，首次实现并行序列生成来解决跨多种维度和物理变量的偏微分方程（PDE）解算子学习问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前深度神经网络在偏微分方程（PDE）解算子学习领域展现出巨大潜力，但现有方法难以统一处理不同维度和物理变量的PDE。自回归PDE基础模型存在效率瓶颈，亟需开发一种可扩展、能并行生成且具有跨场景泛化能力的统一算子学习方法。

Method: 提出了基于条件流匹配（conditional flow-matching）和扩散Transformer（diffusion Transformer）的UniFluids框架。方法包含两个核心创新：一是引入统一的四维时空表示法来处理异构PDE数据集，实现联合训练和条件编码；二是发现PDE数据集的有效维度远低于其补丁维度，因此采用$x$-prediction策略进行流匹配算子学习以提升预测精度。

Result: 在覆盖1D、2D和3D空间维度的多个PDE数据集上进行了大规模评估。实验结果表明，UniFluids在预测精度上表现优异，同时展现出良好的可扩展性和跨场景泛化能力。

Conclusion: UniFluids成功实现了首个基于流匹配的并行序列生成PDE统一算子学习方法，为解决异构PDE问题提供了有效途径。该方法不仅在预测精度上取得显著成果，更证明了统一框架在处理多维度PDE问题上的可行性和优越性。

Abstract: Partial differential equation (PDE) simulation holds extensive significance in scientific research. Currently, the integration of deep neural networks to learn solution operators of PDEs has introduced great potential. In this paper, we present UniFluids, a conditional flow-matching framework that harnesses the scalability of diffusion Transformer to unify learning of solution operators across diverse PDEs with varying dimensionality and physical variables. Unlike the autoregressive PDE foundation models, UniFluids adopts flow-matching to achieve parallel sequence generation, making it the first such approach for unified operator learning. Specifically, the introduction of a unified four-dimensional spatiotemporal representation for the heterogeneous PDE datasets enables joint training and conditional encoding. Furthermore, we find the effective dimension of the PDE dataset is much lower than its patch dimension. We thus employ $x$-prediction in the flow-matching operator learning, which is verified to significantly improve prediction accuracy. We conduct a large-scale evaluation of UniFluids on several PDE datasets covering spatial dimensions 1D, 2D and 3D. Experimental results show that UniFluids achieves strong prediction accuracy and demonstrates good scalability and cross-scenario generalization capability. The code will be released later.

</details>


### [25] [Cloud-Edge Collaborative Large Models for Robust Photovoltaic Power Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22343)
*Nan Qiao,Sijing Duan,Shuning Wang,Xingyuan Hua,Ju Ren*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种风险感知的云边协同光伏预测框架，通过轻量级筛选模块评估风险，并利用Lyapunov引导的路由器在边缘小模型与云端大模型之间动态选择，在满足延迟约束的同时实现了预测精度、鲁棒性与系统效率的良好平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 边缘智能电网中的光伏预测面临预测精度、天气分布偏移下的鲁棒性以及严格延迟约束之间的权衡挑战。本地专用模型在常规条件下高效，但在罕见斜坡事件和未见天气模式下性能下降；而完全依赖云端大模型会带来巨大的通信延迟和云端开销。

Method: 提出风险感知的云边协同框架，集成站点特定专家预测器、轻量级边缘侧增强模型和云端大型检索模型。轻量级筛选模块估计预测不确定性、分布外风险、天气突变强度和模型分歧；Lyapunov引导的路由器在长期延迟、通信和云端使用约束下选择性地将推理升级到边缘或云端分支；通过自适应融合组合激活分支的输出。

Result: 在两个真实光伏数据集上的实验表明，该框架在预测精度、路由质量、鲁棒性和系统效率之间取得了良好的整体权衡。

Conclusion: 所提出的风险感知云边协同框架能够有效应对天气驱动的分布偏移和罕见事件，在满足延迟敏感需求的同时，成功平衡了预测性能与系统资源消耗，为边缘智能电网中的光伏预测提供了实用解决方案。

Abstract: Photovoltaic (PV) power forecasting in edge-enabled grids requires balancing forecasting accuracy, robustness under weather-driven distribution shifts, and strict latency constraints. Local specialized models are efficient for routine conditions but often degrade under rare ramp events and unseen weather patterns, whereas always relying on cloud-side large models incurs substantial communication delay and cloud overhead. To address this challenge, we propose a risk-aware cloud-edge collaborative framework for latency-sensitive PV forecasting. The framework integrates a site-specific expert predictor for routine cases, a lightweight edge-side model for enhanced local inference, and a cloud-side large retrieval model that provides matched historical context when needed through a retrieval-prediction pipeline. A lightweight screening module estimates predictive uncertainty, out-of-distribution risk, weather mutation intensity, and model disagreement, while a Lyapunov-guided router selectively escalates inference to the edge-small or cloud-assisted branches under long-term latency, communication, and cloud-usage constraints. The outputs of the activated branches are combined through adaptive fusion. Experiments on two real-world PV datasets demonstrate a favorable overall trade-off among forecasting accuracy, routing quality, robustness, and system efficiency.

</details>


### [26] [A Multi-Modal CNN-LSTM Framework with Multi-Head Attention and Focal Loss for Real-Time Elderly Fall Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22313)
*Lijie Zhou,Luran Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个名为MultiModalFallDetector的多模态深度学习框架，用于老年人的实时跌倒检测。该框架融合了加速度计、陀螺仪和生理信号，通过多尺度CNN、自注意力机制和辅助任务等技术，在SisFall数据集上达到了98.7%的F1分数，并具备低于50ms的边缘设备推理延迟，适合实时部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全球人口老龄化加剧了对可靠健康监测系统的需求，特别是针对老年人跌倒检测。传统的基于单模态加速度数据的跌倒检测方法存在高误报率问题，而传统的机器学习方法则需要大量手工特征工程，因此迫切需要一种更准确、更自动化的实时跌倒检测解决方案。

Method: 提出MultiModalFallDetector多模态深度学习框架，主要技术包括：(1) 多尺度CNN特征提取器捕捉不同时间分辨率的运动动态；(2) 融合三轴加速度计、陀螺仪和四通道生理信号的多模态数据；(3) 引入多头自注意力机制进行动态时间加权；(4) 采用Focal Loss缓解严重的类别不平衡问题；(5) 引入辅助活动分类任务进行正则化；(6) 实现从UCI HAR到SisFall数据集的迁移学习。

Result: 在包含老年人真实模拟跌倒试验的SisFall数据集上进行广泛实验，该框架实现了98.7%的F1分数、98.9%的召回率和99.4%的AUC-ROC，显著优于包括传统机器学习和标准深度学习方法在内的基线方法。模型在边缘设备上保持低于50ms的推理延迟，验证了其在老年人护理环境中实时部署的可行性。

Conclusion: MultiModalFallDetector框架成功解决了传统跌倒检测方法的局限性，通过多模态融合和深度学习技术实现了高精度、低延迟的实时跌倒检测，为老年人护理和健康监测系统提供了有效的技术解决方案，具有重要的实际应用价值。

Abstract: The increasing global aging population has intensified the demand for reliable health monitoring systems, particularly those capable of detecting critical events such as falls among elderly individuals. Traditional fall detection approaches relying on single-modality acceleration data suffer from high false alarm rates, while conventional machine learning methods require extensive hand-crafted feature engineering. This paper proposes a novel multi-modal deep learning framework, MultiModalFallDetector, designed for real-time elderly fall detection using wearable sensors. Our approach integrates multiple innovations: a multi-scale CNN-based feature extractor capturing motion dynamics at varying temporal resolutions; fusion of tri-axial accelerometer, gyroscope, and four-channel physiological signals; incorporation of a multi-head self-attention mechanism for dynamic temporal weighting; adoption of Focal Loss to mitigate severe class imbalance; introduction of an auxiliary activity classification task for regularization; and implementation of transfer learning from UCI HAR to SisFall dataset. Extensive experiments on the SisFall dataset, which includes real-world simulated fall trials from elderly participants (aged 60-85), demonstrate that our framework achieves an F1-score of 98. 7, Recall of 98. 9, and AUC-ROC of 99. 4, significantly outperforming baseline methods including traditional machine learning and standard deep learning approaches. The model maintains sub- 50ms inference latency on edge devices, confirming its suitability for real-time deployment in geriatric care settings.

</details>

*Peisong Niu,Haifan Zhang,Yang Zhao,Tian Zhou,Ziqing Ma,Wenqiang Shen,Junping Zhao,Huiling Yuan,Liang Sun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出BaguanCyclone框架，通过概率中心细化模块和区域感知强度预测模块，解决了AI天气预报中热带气旋路径和强度预测的关键限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI天气预报系统在热带气旋预测方面存在两大关键缺陷：(1)训练数据为粗分辨率再分析数据（如ERA5的0.25度），将预测的TC位置限制在固定网格上，引入显著的离散化误差；(2)强度预测受限于粗气象场的平滑效应及回归损失导致的预测偏向条件均值，尤其对强热带气旋预测能力有限。

Method: BaguanCyclone框架整合两个核心创新模块：(1)概率中心细化模块，对TC中心的连续空间分布进行建模，实现更精细的路径精度；(2)区域感知强度预测模块，利用TC核心周围动态定义的子网格区域内的高分辨率内部表征，更好地捕捉局地极端值。

Result: 在全球IBTrACS数据集的六个主要热带气旋盆地评估中，系统持续优于业务数值天气预报模型和多数AI基线方法，在重新增强、扫掠弧线、双气旋和蜿蜒事件等复杂气象情景中均表现优异，显著提升了预测精度。

Conclusion: BaguanCyclone成功突破了AI天气预报在热带气旋预测中的分辨率限制和强度预测偏差问题，为热带和亚热带地区的防灾减灾提供了更准确及时的路径和强度预报工具。

Abstract: Tropical cyclones (TCs) pose severe threats to life, infrastructure, and economies in tropical and subtropical regions, underscoring the critical need for accurate and timely forecasts of both track and intensity. Recent advances in AI-based weather forecasting have shown promise in improving TC track forecasts. However, these systems are typically trained on coarse-resolution reanalysis data (e.g., ERA5 at 0.25 degree), which constrains predicted TC positions to a fixed grid and introduces significant discretization errors. Moreover, intensity forecasting remains limited especially for strong TCs by the smoothing effect of coarse meteorological fields and the use of regression losses that bias predictions toward conditional means. To address these limitations, we propose BaguanCyclone, a novel, unified framework that integrates two key innovations: (1) a probabilistic center refinement module that models the continuous spatial distribution of TC centers, enabling finer track precision; and (2) a region-aware intensity forecasting module that leverages high-resolution internal representations within dynamically defined sub-grid zones around the TC core to better capture localized extremes. Evaluated on the global IBTrACS dataset across six major TC basins, our system consistently outperforms both operational numerical weather prediction (NWP) models and most AI-based baselines, delivering a substantial enhancement in forecast accuracy. Remarkably, BaguanCyclone excels in navigating meteorological complexities, consistently delivering accurate forecasts for re-intensification, sweeping arcs, twin cyclones, and meandering events. Our code is available at https://github.com/DAMO-DI-ML/Baguan-cyclone.

</details>


### [28] [Emergency Preemption Without Online Exploration: A Decision Transformer Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22315)
*Haoran Su,Hanxiao Deng,Yandong Sun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于决策Transformer的紧急车辆走廊优化框架，通过返回条件化的离线序列建模方法，在无需在线环境交互的情况下实现了紧急车辆通行时间的显著降低，并提供了紧急程度与平民延误之间的可调节权衡机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 紧急车辆响应时间是决定生存结果的关键因素，然而现有的信号优先策略仍然是被动且不可控的，缺乏对紧急程度的灵活调节能力，且传统在线强化学习方法需要环境交互，在实际部署中存在困难。

Method: 将走廊优化问题建模为离线、返回条件化的序列建模任务；提出Decision Transformer (DT)方法，通过单一目标返回标量实现调度级别的紧急程度控制；扩展至多智能体场景，提出基于图注意力机制的多智能体决策Transformer (MADT)实现空间协调；引入约束DT扩展，将平民干扰预算作为第二控制参数。

Result: 在LightSim仿真器上，DT在4x4网格上将紧急车辆平均通行时间相比固定时序优先降低37.7%（88.6秒 vs. 142.3秒），在所有方法中实现最低平民延误（11.3秒/车）和最少紧急车辆停车次数（1.2次）；MADT在更大规模网格上表现更优，在8x8网格上通过图注意力协调实现45.2%的降低；返回条件化提供了平滑的调度界面，目标返回值从100到-400变化可在无需重训练的情况下权衡紧急车辆通行时间（72.4-138.2秒）与平民延误（16.8-5.4秒/车）。

Conclusion: 基于决策Transformer的返回条件化框架成功实现了紧急车辆走廊优化，在无需在线环境交互的前提下超越了在线强化学习基线方法的性能，同时提供了灵活的紧急程度-平民延误权衡控制，为实际紧急车辆调度系统提供了实用可行的解决方案。

Abstract: Emergency vehicle (EV) response time is a critical determinant of survival outcomes, yet deployed signal preemption strategies remain reactive and uncontrollable. We propose a return-conditioned framework for emergency corridor optimization based on the Decision Transformer (DT). By casting corridor optimization as offline, return-conditioned sequence modeling, our approach (1) eliminates online environment interaction during policy learning, (2) enables dispatch-level urgency control through a single target-return scalar, and (3) extends to multi-agent settings via a Multi-Agent Decision Transformer (MADT) with graph attention for spatial coordination. On the LightSim simulator, DT reduces average EV travel time by 37.7% relative to fixed-timing preemption on a 4x4 grid (88.6 s vs. 142.3 s), achieving the lowest civilian delay (11.3 s/veh) and fewest EV stops (1.2) among all methods, including online RL baselines that require environment interaction. MADT further improves on larger grids, overtaking DT with 45.2% reduction on 8x8 via graph-attention coordination. Return conditioning produces a smooth dispatch interface: varying the target return from 100 to -400 trades EV travel time (72.4-138.2 s) against civilian delay (16.8-5.4 s/veh), requiring no retraining. A Constrained DT extension adds explicit civilian disruption budgets as a second control knob.

</details>


### [29] [ST-GDance++: A Scalable Spatial-Temporal Diffusion for Long-Duration Group Choreography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22316)
*Jing Xu,Weiqiang Wang,Cunjian Chen,Jun Liu,Qiuhong Ke*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出ST-GDance++框架，通过解耦时空依赖实现高效、可扩展的群舞生成，在保持生成质量的同时显著降低延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多人群舞生成模型依赖双向注意力机制，随着舞者数量和序列长度增加，计算复杂度呈二次增长，导致效率低下且易发生运动碰撞，难以应用于交互场景。

Method: 提出ST-GDance++框架，采用时空解耦策略：空间上引入轻量级距离感知图卷积建模舞者间关系；时间上设计扩散噪声调度策略和高效时间对齐注意力掩码，支持长序列流式生成。

Result: 在AIOZ-GDance数据集上的实验表明，该方法在保持竞争性生成质量的同时，相比现有方法显著降低了生成延迟。

Conclusion: 该框架有效解决了群舞生成的可扩展性和多舞者协调问题，为交互式应用提供了可行的解决方案。

Abstract: Group dance generation from music requires synchronizing multiple dancers while maintaining spatial coordination, making it highly relevant to applications such as film production, gaming, and animation. Recent group dance generation models have achieved promising generation quality, but they remain difficult to deploy in interactive scenarios due to bidirectional attention dependencies. As the number of dancers and the sequence length increase, the attention computation required for aligning music conditions with motion sequences grows quadratically, leading to reduced efficiency and increased risk of motion collisions. Effectively modeling dense spatial-temporal interactions is therefore essential, yet existing methods often struggle to capture such complexity, resulting in limited scalability and unstable multi-dancer coordination. To address these challenges, we propose ST-GDance++, a scalable framework that decouples spatial and temporal dependencies to enable efficient and collision-aware group choreography generation. For spatial modeling, we introduce lightweight distance-aware graph convolutions to capture inter-dancer relationships while reducing computational overhead. For temporal modeling, we design a diffusion noise scheduling strategy together with an efficient temporal-aligned attention mask, enabling stream-based generation for long motion sequences and improving scalability in long-duration scenarios. Experiments on the AIOZ-GDance dataset show that ST-GDance++ achieves competitive generation quality with significantly reduced latency compared to existing methods.

</details>


### [30] [Geometric Mixture-of-Experts with Curvature-Guided Adaptive Routing for Graph Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22317)
*Haifang Cao,Yu Wang,Timing Li,Xinjie Yao,Pengfei Zhu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了GeoMoE框架，利用Ollivier-Ricci曲率作为几何先验，自适应融合不同黎曼空间中的节点表示，解决了图结构数据拓扑异质性建模难题，在六个基准数据集上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图结构数据通常表现出复杂的拓扑异质性，难以在单一黎曼流形内进行准确建模；现有的混合曲率方法依赖隐式的、任务驱动的路由机制，缺乏根本的几何基础。

Method: 提出几何混合专家框架（GeoMoE），利用Ollivier-Ricci曲率（ORC）作为内在几何先验来协调专家协作；设计图感知门控网络分配节点特定的融合权重，并通过曲率引导的对齐损失进行正则化；引入曲率感知对比目标，根据曲率一致性构建正负样本对以促进几何判别性。

Result: 在六个基准数据集上的广泛实验表明，GeoMoE在多种图类型上均优于最先进的基线方法。

Conclusion: GeoMoE通过将曲率作为几何先验融入混合专家路由机制，成功实现了可解释且几何一致的路由，有效捕获了图数据的多尺度拓扑结构，为异质图表示学习提供了新思路。

Abstract: Graph-structured data typically exhibits complex topological heterogeneity, making it difficult to model accurately within a single Riemannian manifold. While emerging mixed-curvature methods attempt to capture such diversity, they often rely on implicit, task-driven routing that lacks fundamental geometric grounding. To address this challenge, we propose a Geometric Mixture-of-Experts framework (GeoMoE) that adaptively fuses node representations across diverse Riemannian spaces to better accommodate multi-scale topological structures. At its core, GeoMoE leverages Ollivier-Ricci Curvature (ORC) as an intrinsic geometric prior to orchestrate the collaboration of specialized experts. Specifically, we design a graph-aware gating network that assigns node-specific fusion weights, regularized by a curvature-guided alignment loss to ensure interpretable and geometry-consistent routing. Additionally, we introduce a curvature-aware contrastive objective that promotes geometric discriminability by constructing positive and negative pairs according to curvature consistency. Extensive experiments on six benchmark datasets demonstrate that GeoMoE outperforms state-of-the-art baselines across diverse graph types.

</details>


### [31] [A graph neural network based chemical mechanism reduction method for combustion applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22318)
*Manuru Nithin Padiyar,Priyabrat Dash,Konduri Aditya*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了两种基于图神经网络（GNN）和消息传递Transformer层的数据驱动方法（GNN-SM和GNN-AE），用于自动简化复杂的化学机理，在保证精度的同时显著降低了计算成本，其中GNN-AE在目标工况下的简化程度高达95%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对包含数百万网格点和数百种组分、数千反应的湍流反应流进行直接数值模拟（DNS）在计算上极其昂贵且难以承受。为了解决这一挑战，需要开发能够有效简化详细化学机理的方法，以降低计算成本同时保持关键化学特性的准确性。

Method: 提出了两种基于图神经网络（GNN）与消息传递Transformer层的化学机理简化方法：1）GNN-SM，利用预训练的代理模型引导在广泛反应器条件下的简化过程；2）GNN-AE，采用自编码器架构以获得高度紧凑的简化机理，并确保在训练的热化学区域内保持准确性。

Result: 该方法在甲烷（53种组分）、乙烯（96种组分）和异辛烷（1034种组分）的详细机理上进行了验证。GNN-SM在广泛的热化学状态下保持了与传统图方法DRGEP相当的简化效果和精度；而GNN-AE在其目标工况内实现了高达95%的组分和反应简化率，且性能优于DRGEP方法。

Conclusion: 该研究建立的框架提供了一条自动化的、基于机器学习的化学机理简化途径，可以作为传统专家指导分析方法的补充，有效解决复杂化学反应流模拟中的计算瓶颈问题。

Abstract: Direct numerical simulations of turbulent reacting flows involving millions of grid points and detailed chemical mechanisms with hundreds of species and thousands of reactions are computationally prohibitive. To address this challenge, we present two data-driven chemical mechanism reduction formulations based on graph neural networks (GNNs) with message-passing transformer layers that learn nonlinear dependencies among species and reactions. The first formulation, GNN-SM, employs a pre-trained surrogate model to guide reduction across a broad range of reactor conditions. The second formulation, GNN-AE, uses an autoencoder formulation to obtain highly compact mechanisms that remain accurate within the thermochemical regimes used during training. The approaches are demonstrated on detailed mechanisms for methane (53 species, 325 reactions), ethylene (96 species, 1054 reactions), and iso-octane (1034 species, 8453 reactions). GNN-SM achieves reductions comparable to the established graph-based method DRGEP while maintaining accuracy across a wide range of thermochemical states. In contrast, GNN-AE achieves up to 95% reduction in species and reactions and outperforms DRGEP within its target conditions. Overall, the proposed framework provides an automated, machine-learning-based pathway for chemical mechanism reduction that can complement traditional expert-guided analytical approaches.

</details>


### [32] [Sparsely-Supervised Data Assimilation via Physics-Informed Schrödinger Bridge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22319)
*Dohyun Bu,Chanho Kim,Seokun Choi,Jong-Seok Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了物理信息条件薛定谔桥（PICSB）方法，用于解决偏微分方程系统的数据同化问题，能够在稀疏高保真观测下快速准确地重建时空场，无需测试时优化或全高保真监督。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法存在两个主要局限：（1）传统多保真数据同化需要逐实例的测试时优化，在时间敏感应用中成为瓶颈；（2）基于生成模型的摊销重建方法依赖全高保真场监督进行训练，这在现实场景中往往不切实际。因此需要一种既能实现快速推理，又无需全高保真监督的方法。

Method: 提出物理信息条件薛定谔桥（PICSB）框架：（1）将信息性低保真先验传输至观测条件的高保真后验，无需额外推理时引导；（2）采用迭代代理端点刷新方案，实现无高保真端点的学习；（3）将PDE残差直接纳入训练目标；（4）在采样过程中通过硬条件约束强制满足观测条件。

Result: 在流体PDE基准测试上的实验表明，PICSB能够实现极快的时空场重建，同时在稀疏高保真监督下保持具有竞争力的精度。

Conclusion: PICSB成功地在无需全高保真监督和测试时优化的前提下，实现了物理约束的快速时空场重建，为实际应用中的数据同化问题提供了一种实用的解决方案。

Abstract: Data assimilation (DA) for systems governed by partial differential equations (PDE) aims to reconstruct full spatiotemporal fields from sparse high-fidelity (HF) observations while respecting physical constraints. While full-grid low-fidelity (LF) simulations provide informative priors in multi-fidelity settings, recovering an HF field consistent with both sparse observations and the governing PDE typically requires per-instance test-time optimization, which becomes a major bottleneck in time-critical applications. To alleviate this, amortized reconstruction using generative models has recently been proposed; however, such approaches rely on full-field HF supervision during training, which is often impractical in real-world settings. From a more realistic perspective, we propose the Physics-Informed Conditional Schrödinger Bridge (PICSB), which transports an informative LF prior toward an observation-conditioned HF posterior without any additional inference-time guidance. To enable learning without HF endpoints, PICSB employs an iterative surrogate-endpoint refresh scheme, and directly incorporates PDE residuals into the training objective while enforcing observations via hard conditioning throughout sampling. Experiments on fluid PDE benchmarks demonstrate that PICSB enables extremely fast spatiotemporal field reconstruction while maintaining competitive accuracy under sparse HF supervision.

</details>


### [33] [Bridging the Gap Between Climate Science and Machine Learning in Climate Model Emulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22320)
*Luca Schmidt,Nina Effenberger*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个整合气候科学和机器学习视角的框架，通过设计易于采用的任务导向型模拟器并验证其可靠性，以克服气候模型机器学习替代方案在应用中面临的技术门槛高、专业知识缺乏和可信度不足等障碍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 气候模型虽能为气候决策提供洞见，但受限于巨大的计算和技术需求。尽管机器学习模拟器可规避高计算成本，但其应用仍面临诸多挑战，包括可及性有限、专业知识缺乏，以及因被认为缺乏物理基础而产生的普遍不信任感。

Method: 提出一个整合气候科学与机器学习双重视角的框架，强调设计易于采用、针对明确任务的模拟器，并通过展示其可靠性来促进两个领域之间的融合。

Result: 研究发现，设计易于采用的任务导向型模拟器并验证其可靠性，是弥合气候科学与机器学习领域之间鸿沟的一条可行路径。

Conclusion: 通过整合领域知识、降低技术门槛并建立信任，可以有效推动机器学习模拟器在气候科学中的应用，从而为气候决策提供更高效的计算支持。

Abstract: While climate models provide insights for climate decision-making, their use is constrained by significant computational and technical demands. Although machine learning (ML) emulators offer a way to bypass the high computational costs, their effective use remains challenging. The hurdles are diverse, ranging from limited accessibility and a lack of specialized knowledge to a general mistrust of ML methods that are perceived as insufficiently physical. Here, we introduce a framework to overcome these barriers by integrating both climate science and machine learning perspectives. We find that designing easy-to-adopt emulators that address a clearly defined task and demonstrating their reliability offers a promising path for bridging the gap between our two fields.

</details>


### [34] [AEGIS: An Operational Infrastructure for Post-Market Governance of Adaptive Medical AI Under US and EU Regulations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22322)
*Fardin Afdideh,Mehdi Astaraki,Fernando Seoane,Farhad Abtahi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了AEGIS框架，通过数据集同化与重训练、模型监控和条件决策三个模块，将FDA PCCP和欧盟AI法案的监管要求转化为可执行的治理流程，支持自适应医疗AI系统的安全持续学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医疗设备中的机器学习系统需要治理框架来确保安全性并支持持续改进，而监管机构已引入预定变更控制计划（PCCP）和上市后监督（PMS）等机制来管理迭代模型更新，但这些概念需要转化为可执行的治理程序。

Method: 提出AEGIS治理框架，包含三个核心模块：数据集同化与重训练、模型监控、条件决策。实施四类部署决策分类法（批准、条件批准、临床审查、拒绝），并配备独立的PMS警报信号，用于检测关键状态——即不存在可部署模型且已发布模型同时面临风险的情况。通过脓毒症预测和脑肿瘤分割两个临床场景进行验证。

Result: 在脓毒症预测案例的11次模拟迭代中，AEGIS产生了8次批准、1次条件批准、1次临床审查和1次拒绝决策，覆盖所有四个类别。在第8和第10次迭代中发出警报信号，包括检测到无可用模型且已发布模型同时失效的关键状态。AEGIS在性能退化可观察之前就检测到了漂移。

Conclusion: AEGIS成功将监管变更控制概念转化为可执行的治理程序，能够支持跨不同临床应用的自适应医疗AI的安全持续学习，为医疗AI治理提供了实用的技术框架。

Abstract: Machine learning systems deployed in medical devices require governance frameworks that ensure safety while enabling continuous improvement. Regulatory bodies including the FDA and European Union have introduced mechanisms such as the Predetermined Change Control Plan (PCCP) and Post-Market Surveillance (PMS) to manage iterative model updates without repeated submissions. This paper presents AI/ML Evaluation and Governance Infrastructure for Safety (AEGIS), a governance framework applicable to any healthcare AI system. AEGIS comprises three modules, i.e., dataset assimilation and retraining, model monitoring, and conditional decision, that operationalize FDA PCCP and EU AI Act Article 43(4) provisions. We implement a four-category deployment decision taxonomy (APPROVE, CONDITIONAL APPROVAL, CLINICAL REVIEW, REJECT) with an independent PMS ALARM signal, enabling detection of the critical state in which no deployable model exists while the released model is simultaneously at risk.
  To illustrate how AEGIS can be instantiated across heterogeneous clinical contexts, we provide two examples: sepsis prediction from electronic health records and brain tumor segmentation from medical imaging. Both cases use identical governance architecture, differing only in configuration. Across 11 simulated iterations on the sepsis example, AEGIS yielded 8 APPROVE, 1 CONDITIONAL APPROVAL, 1 CLINICAL REVIEW, and 1 REJECT decision, exercising all four categories. ALARM signals were co-issued at iterations 8 and 10, including the critical state where no deployable model exists and the released model is simultaneously failing. AEGIS detected drift before observable performance degradation. These results demonstrate that AEGIS translates regulatory change-control concepts into executable governance procedures, supporting safe continuous learning for adaptive medical AI across diverse clinical applications.

</details>

Motivation: 准确预测锂离子电池的SOH和RUL对于确保电动汽车安全高效运行至关重要。然而，现有深度学习方法在选择性特征提取和时间依赖建模方面能力有限，且传统循环神经网络在长期时间序列建模中存在固有缺陷，亟需改进。

Method: 提出多任务目标学习框架，包含三个核心模块：(1)多尺度CNN特征提取模块捕获局部电池衰减模式；(2)改进的扩展LSTM网络增强长期时间信息保持能力；(3)双流注意力模块（包括极化注意力和稀疏注意力）分别对SOH和RUL关键信息选择性聚焦。通过双任务层实现多对二映射，并采用Hyperopt算法优化超参数。

Result: 在电池老化数据集上的大量对比实验表明，与传统和最先进方法相比，所提方法的SOH和RUL预测平均RMSE分别降低了111.3%和33.0%，显著提升了预测精度。

Abstract: Accurately predicting the state-of-health (SOH) and remaining useful life (RUL) of lithium-ion batteries is crucial for ensuring the safe and efficient operation of electric vehicles while minimizing associated risks. However, current deep learning methods are limited in their ability to selectively extract features and model time dependencies for these two parameters. Moreover, most existing methods rely on traditional recurrent neural networks, which have inherent shortcomings in long-term time-series modeling. To address these issues, this paper proposes a multi-task targeted learning framework for SOH and RUL prediction, which integrates multiple neural networks, including a multi-scale feature extraction module, an improved extended LSTM, and a dual-stream attention module. First, a feature extraction module with multi-scale CNNs is designed to capture detailed local battery decline patterns. Secondly, an improved extended LSTM network is employed to enhance the model's ability to retain long-term temporal information, thus improving temporal relationship modeling. Building on this, the dual-stream attention module-comprising polarized attention and sparse attention to selectively focus on key information relevant to SOH and RUL, respectively, by assigning higher weights to important features. Finally, a many-to-two mapping is achieved through the dual-task layer. To optimize the model's performance and reduce the need for manual hyperparameter tuning, the Hyperopt optimization algorithm is used. Extensive comparative experiments on battery aging datasets demonstrate that the proposed method reduces the average RMSE for SOH and RUL predictions by 111.3\% and 33.0\%, respectively, compared to traditional and state-of-the-art methods.

</details>


### [36] [DAQ: Delta-Aware Quantization for Post-Training LLM Weight Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22324)
*Xiaoming Yu,Shize Tang,Guanghua Yu,Linchuan Xie,Song Liu,Jianchen Zhu,Feng Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了Delta-Aware Quantization (DAQ)，一种数据无关的训练后量化框架，通过保持参数增量(ΔW)的方向保真度，解决了标准量化方法会破坏训练后所学知识的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准量化方法通过最小化重建误差进行优化，但忽略了对基础模型的感知，导致量化噪声不成比例地破坏编码训练后行为的小幅度参数增量(ΔW)，从而使训练后获得的知识丢失。

Method: 提出Delta-Aware Quantization (DAQ)框架，用两个增量感知指标——符号保持率(Sign Preservation Rate)和余弦相似度(Cosine Similarity)——替代基于重建的目标函数，直接优化ΔW的方向保真度，该方法仅需基础权重矩阵和训练后权重矩阵，无需额外数据。

Result: 在FP8实验研究中，DAQ成功恢复了标准量化丢失的风格特定能力，同时保持了通用性能表现。

Conclusion: DAQ通过显式优化参数增量的方向保真度，有效解决了训练后量化中的知识保持问题，为数据无关的训练后量化提供了新思路。

Abstract: We introduce Delta-Aware Quantization (DAQ), a data-free post-training quantization framework that preserves the knowledge acquired during post-training. Standard quantization objectives minimize reconstruction error but are agnostic to the base model, allowing quantization noise to disproportionately corrupt the small-magnitude parameter deltas ($ΔW$) that encode post-training behavior -- an effect we analyze through the lens of quantization as implicit regularization. DAQ replaces reconstruction-based objectives with two delta-aware metrics -- Sign Preservation Rate and Cosine Similarity -- that directly optimize for directional fidelity of $ΔW$, requiring only the base and post-trained weight matrices. In a pilot FP8 study, DAQ recovers style-specific capabilities lost under standard quantization while maintaining general performance.

</details>


### [37] [Hybrid Associative Memories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22325)
*Leon Lufkin,Tomás Figliolia,Beren Millidge,Kamesh Krishnamurthy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出HAM层，结合RNN和自注意力，按需增长KV缓存，在低存储成本下实现竞争力性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RNN与自注意力在记忆机制上互补：RNN将历史压缩为固定状态，效率高但长上下文性能下降；自注意力存储所有历史，记忆精准但内存和计算成本随序列线性增长。现有结合方式多为简单交替，未能充分利用两者优势。

Method: 提出混合关联记忆（HAM）层：RNN压缩整个序列，注意力仅补充RNN难以预测的信息（即最有价值的信息），实现数据依赖的KV缓存增长，用户可通过单一连续阈值精确控制缓存大小。

Result: KV缓存增长率的精细控制与损失和性能之间存在平滑权衡；在显著降低KV缓存使用量的情况下，混合架构相比RNN和Transformer仍具竞争力表现。

Conclusion: HAM层有效结合了RNN的效率与自注意力的精准记忆能力，实现了性能与资源消耗之间的可控平衡。

Abstract: Recurrent neural networks (RNNs) and self-attention are both widely used sequence-mixing layers that maintain an internal memory. However, this memory is constructed using two orthogonal mechanisms: RNNs compress the entire past into a fixed-size state, whereas self-attention's state stores every past time step growing its state (the KV cache) linearly with the sequence length. This results in orthogonal strengths and weaknesses. Self-attention layers excel at retrieving information in the context but have large memory and computational costs, while RNNs are more efficient but degrade over longer contexts and underperform for precise recall tasks. Prior work combining these mechanisms has focused primarily on naively interleaving them to reduce computational cost without regard to their complementary mechanisms. We propose the Hybrid Associative Memory (HAM) layer, which combines self-attention and RNNs while leveraging their individual strengths: the RNN compresses the entire sequence, while attention supplements it *only* with information that is difficult for the RNN to predict, which is hence the most valuable information to explicitly store. HAM layers enable data-dependent growth of the KV cache, which can be precisely controlled by the user with a single, continuous threshold. We find that this fine-grained control of the KV cache growth rate has a smooth trade-off with loss and performance. Empirically, we show that our hybrid architecture offers strong, competitive performance relative to RNNs and Transformers even at substantially lower KV-cache usage.

</details>

*Alejandro Morales-Hernández,Fabrizio De Caroa,Gian Marco Paldino,Pascal Tribel,Alfredo Vaccaro,Gianluca Bontempi*
