Existing protection strategies rely on rigid, uniform defense that prevent dynamic authorization. Structural isolation methods faces scalability bottlenecks, while prompt guidance methods struggle with fine-grained permissions distinctions. Here, we propose the Chain-of-Authorization (CoA) framework, a secure training and reasoning paradigm that internalizes authorization logic into LLMs' core capabilities. Unlike passive external defneses, CoA restructures the model's information flow: it embeds permission context at input and requires generating explicit authorization reasoning trajectory that includes resource review, identity resolution, and decision-making stages before final response. Through supervised fine-tuning on data covering various authorization status, CoA integrates policy execution with task responses, making authorization a causal prerequisite for substantive responses. Extensive evaluations show that CoA not only maintains comparable utility in authorized scenarios but also overcomes the cognitive confusion when permissions mismatches. It exhibits high rejection rates against various unauthorized and adversarial access. This mechanism leverages LLMs' reasoning capability to perform dynamic authorization, using natural language understanding as a proactive security mechanism for deploying reliable LLMs in modern AI systems.

</details>


### [146] [Dynamical Systems Theory Behind a Hierarchical Reasoning Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22871)
*Vasiliy A. Es'kin,Mikhail E. Smorkalov*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出收缩映射模型（CMM），将离散递归推理重构为连续神经（随机）微分方程，以数学严格的方式解决LLM在复杂算法推理中的不足，实现了极致的参数效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大语言模型主要依赖线性序列生成和海量参数，在复杂算法推理任务上表现严重不足；而现有的紧凑递归架构（如HRM和TRM）虽然能应对此类任务，但其训练动态缺乏严格的数学保证，常导致不稳定和表示坍塌。

Method: 提出收缩映射模型（CMM），将离散递归推理形式化为连续的神经常微分方程（NODEs）和神经随机微分方程（NSDEs）。通过显式约束潜在相点收敛至稳定平衡态，并引入超球面排斥损失来缓解特征坍塌，从而构建一个具有严格数学理论支撑且高度稳定的推理引擎。

Result: 在Sudoku-Extreme基准上，5M参数的CMM达到93.7%的SOTA准确率，显著优于27M参数的HRM（55.0%）和5M参数的TRM（87.4%）。当被极致压缩至仅0.26M参数时，CMM仍能在Sudoku-Extreme上保持85.4%的准确率，并在Maze基准上达到82.2%。

Conclusion: 该研究证明了数学上严格的潜在动力学可以有效替代暴力扩展，为极致参数效率建立了新的前沿，表明紧凑且理论严谨的架构在人工推理任务中具有巨大潜力。

Abstract: Current large language models (LLMs) primarily rely on linear sequence generation and massive parameter counts, yet they severely struggle with complex algorithmic reasoning. While recent reasoning architectures, such as the Hierarchical Reasoning Model (HRM) and Tiny Recursive Model (TRM), demonstrate that compact recursive networks can tackle these tasks, their training dynamics often lack rigorous mathematical guarantees, leading to instability and representational collapse. We propose the Contraction Mapping Model (CMM), a novel architecture that reformulates discrete recursive reasoning into continuous Neural Ordinary and Stochastic Differential Equations (NODEs/NSDEs). By explicitly enforcing the convergence of the latent phase point to a stable equilibrium state and mitigating feature collapse with a hyperspherical repulsion loss, the CMM provides a mathematically grounded and highly stable reasoning engine. On the Sudoku-Extreme benchmark, a 5M-parameter CMM achieves a state-of-the-art accuracy of 93.7 %, outperforming the 27M-parameter HRM (55.0 %) and 5M-parameter TRM (87.4 %). Remarkably, even when aggressively compressed to an ultra-tiny footprint of just 0.26M parameters, the CMM retains robust predictive power, achieving 85.4 % on Sudoku-Extreme and 82.2 % on the Maze benchmark. These results establish a new frontier for extreme parameter efficiency, proving that mathematically rigorous latent dynamics can effectively replace brute-force scaling in artificial reasoning.

</details>


### [147] [Continuous Optimization for Satisfiability Modulo Theories on Linear Real Arithmetic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22877)
*Yunuo Cen,Daniel Ebler,Xuanyao Fong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了FourierSMT，一种可扩展、高度可并行化的SMT连续变量优化框架，在大规模问题上相比现有SMT求解器实现8倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有SMT求解方法主要基于冲突驱动子句学习（CDCL），其结构难以并行化，扩展性差，无法满足硬件验证等工业应用对大规模问题求解的需求。

Method: 将Walsh-Fourier展开（WFE）从布尔域推广至混合布尔-实数域（xWFE），使SMT问题可使用梯度方法求解；提出扩展二叉决策图（xBDD）降低xWFE评估复杂度，证明xBDD在随机舍入下的电路输出概率采样等价于xWFE的期望值，实现高效约束计算。

Result: 在包含多达10000个变量和700000个约束的大规模调度与布局问题上进行验证，相比最先进SMT求解器实现8倍加速，证明算法收敛且保持可满足性。

Conclusion: FourierSMT为基于GPU的SMT连续系统优化开辟了新途径，证明了将连续优化方法应用于SMT问题的可行性与高效性。

Abstract: Efficient solutions for satisfiability modulo theories (SMT) are integral in industrial applications such as hardware verification and design automation. Existing approaches are predominantly based on conflict-driven clause learning, which is structurally difficult to parallelize and therefore scales poorly. In this work, we introduce FourierSMT as a scalable and highly parallelizable continuous-variable optimization framework for SMT. We generalize the Walsh-Fourier expansion (WFE), called extended WFE (xWFE), from the Boolean domain to a mixed Boolean-real domain, which allows the use of gradient methods for SMT. This addresses the challenge of finding satisfying variable assignments to high-arity constraints by local updates of discrete variables. To reduce the evaluation complexity of xWFE, we present the extended binary decision diagram (xBDD) and map the constraints from xWFE to xBDDs. We then show that sampling the circuit-output probability (COP) of xBDDs under randomized rounding is equivalent to the expectation value of the xWFEs. This allows for efficient computation of the constraints. We show that the reduced problem is guaranteed to converge and preserves satisfiability, ensuring the soundness of the solutions. The framework is benchmarked for large-scale scheduling and placement problems with up to 10,000 variables and 700,000 constraints, achieving 8-fold speedups compared to state-of-the-art SMT solvers. These results pave the way for GPU-based optimization of SMTs with continuous systems.

</details>


### [148] [Separating Diagnosis from Control: Auditable Policy Adaptation in Agent-Based Simulations with LLM-Based Diagnostics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22904)
*Shaoxin Zhong,Yuchen Su,Michael Witbrock*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出三层框架解决老年人孤独感干预政策中适应性与可审计性难以兼得的问题。通过LLM诊断与确定性控制分离，在透明度与性能间取得平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 缓解老年人孤独感的政策干预需要同时具备适应性和可审计性，但现有方法存在矛盾：传统基于智能体的模型存在静态刚性，缺乏灵活性；而直接使用大语言模型(LLM)作为控制器虽然具备适应性，但缺乏必要的可追溯性。如何同时实现这两个目标成为亟待解决的问题。

Method: 提出三层框架，实现诊断与控制的分离：LLM严格作为诊断工具，评估人群状态并生成结构化风险评价；确定性公式与显式边界将这些评估转化为可追溯的参数更新。这种分离确保每个政策决策都可归因于可检查的规则，同时保持对新兴需求的适应性响应。通过五个实验条件下的系统消融实验验证框架有效性。

Result: 显式控制规则在保持完全可审计性的同时，性能比端到端黑盒LLM方法提升11.7%，证明透明性无需以牺牲适应性为代价。

Conclusion: 通过将LLM的诊断能力与确定性控制规则相结合，该框架成功实现了适应性与可审计性的统一，为老年人护理模拟中的政策干预提供了既透明又高效的技术路径。

Abstract: Mitigating elderly loneliness requires policy interventions that achieve both adaptability and auditability. Existing methods struggle to reconcile these objectives: traditional agent-based models suffer from static rigidity, while direct large language model (LLM) controllers lack essential traceability. This work proposes a three-layer framework that separates diagnosis from control to achieve both properties simultaneously. LLMs operate strictly as diagnostic instruments that assess population state and generate structured risk evaluations, while deterministic formulas with explicit bounds translate these assessments into traceable parameter updates. This separation ensures that every policy decision can be attributed to inspectable rules while maintaining adaptive response to emergent needs. We validate the framework through systematic ablation across five experimental conditions in elderly care simulation. Results demonstrate that explicit control rules outperform end-to-end black-box LLM approaches by 11.7\% while preserving full auditability, confirming that transparency need not compromise adaptive performance.

</details>


### [149] [ProGRank: Probe-Gradient Reranking to Defend Dense-Retriever RAG from Corpus Poisoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22934)
*Xiangyu Yin,Yi Qi,Chih-hong Cheng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了ProGRank，一种针对密集检索器RAG系统的后处理、免训练检索端防御方法，通过检测查询-段落对的不稳定性信号来有效防御语料库投毒攻击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检索增强生成（RAG）虽然通过引入检索证据提高了大语言模型的可靠性，但也引入了新的攻击面：语料库投毒攻击。攻击者可以通过注入或编辑段落来影响检索结果和下游生成。现有的防御方法通常依赖内容过滤、辅助模型或生成端推理，导致部署困难。

Method: 提出ProGRank方法，这是一种后处理、免训练的检索端防御方法。该方法对每个查询-段落对在轻微随机扰动下进行压力测试，从检索器的小型固定参数子集中提取探测梯度。基于这些信号，提取两个不稳定性信号：表示一致性（representational consistency）和分散风险（dispersion risk），并在重排序步骤中将它们与分数门控结合。该方法保留原始段落内容，无需重训练，并支持当部署的检索器不可用时的代理变体。

Result: 在三个数据集、三个密集检索器主干、代表性语料库投毒攻击以及检索阶段和端到端设置中进行了广泛实验。结果显示ProGRank提供了更强的防御性能和良好的鲁棒性-效用权衡，并在自适应逃避攻击下仍保持竞争力。

Conclusion: ProGRank是一种有效的语料库投毒防御方法，具有易于部署、无需重训练、保持效用等优势，为RAG系统提供了一种实用的安全解决方案。

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) improves the reliability of large language model applications by grounding generation in retrieved evidence, but it also introduces a new attack surface: corpus poisoning. In this setting, an adversary injects or edits passages so that they are ranked into the Top-$K$ results for target queries and then affect downstream generation. Existing defences against corpus poisoning often rely on content filtering, auxiliary models, or generator-side reasoning, which can make deployment more difficult. We propose ProGRank, a post hoc, training-free retriever-side defence for dense-retriever RAG. ProGRank stress-tests each query--passage pair under mild randomized perturbations and extracts probe gradients from a small fixed parameter subset of the retriever. From these signals, it derives two instability signals, representational consistency and dispersion risk, and combines them with a score gate in a reranking step. ProGRank preserves the original passage content, requires no retraining, and also supports a surrogate-based variant when the deployed retriever is unavailable. Extensive experiments across three datasets, three dense retriever backbones, representative corpus poisoning attacks, and both retrieval-stage and end-to-end settings show that ProGRank provides stronger defence performance and a favorable robustness--utility trade-off. It also remains competitive under adaptive evasive attacks.

</details>


### [150] [Ran Score: a LLM-based Evaluation Score for Radiology Report Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22935)
*Ran Zhang,Yucong Lin,Zhaoli Su,Bowen Liu,Danni Ai,Tianyu Fu,Deqiang Xiao,Jingfan Fan,Yuanyuan Wang,Mingwei Gao,Yuwan Hu,Shuya Gao,Jingtao Li,Jian Yang,Hong Song,Hongliang Sun*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个结合临床专家和大型语言模型的框架，用于从胸片报告中提取多标签发现，并定义了Ran Score这一发现级别的报告评估指标，显著提升了对低 prevalence 异常的识别能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的胸片报告生成和自动化评估方法在识别低患病率异常方面表现不佳，且无法充分处理临床上重要的语言表达，包括否定和模糊表述。

Method: 开发了一个临床专家指导的框架，结合人类专业知识和大型语言模型，从自由文本胸片报告中进行多标签发现提取；使用三个非重叠的MIMIC-CXR-EN队列和一个独立的ChestX-CN验证队列，优化提示词，建立放射科医生标注的参考标签，并评估报告生成模型。

Result: 优化后的框架在MIMIC-CXR-EN开发队列上将宏平均分数从0.753提升至0.956，在直接可比标签上超越CheXbert基准15.7个百分点，并在ChestX-CN验证队列上展现出良好的泛化能力。

Conclusion: 临床专家指导的提示词优化能够提高与放射科医生参考标准的一致性，Ran Score实现了发现级别的报告保真度评估，尤其适用于低患病率异常的评估。

Abstract: Chest X-ray report generation and automated evaluation are limited by poor recognition of low-prevalence abnormalities and inadequate handling of clinically important language, including negation and ambiguity. We develop a clinician-guided framework combining human expertise and large language models for multi-label finding extraction from free-text chest X-ray reports and use it to define Ran Score, a finding-level metric for report evaluation. Using three non-overlapping MIMIC-CXR-EN cohorts from a public chest X-ray dataset and an independent ChestX-CN validation cohort, we optimize prompts, establish radiologist-derived reference labels and evaluate report generation models. The optimized framework improves the macro-averaged score from 0.753 to 0.956 on the MIMIC-CXR-EN development cohort, exceeds the CheXbert benchmark by 15.7 percentage points on directly comparable labels, and shows robust generalization on the ChestX-CN validation cohort. Here we show that clinician-guided prompt optimization improves agreement with a radiologist-derived reference standard and that Ran Score enables finding-level evaluation of report fidelity, particularly for low-prevalence abnormalities.

</details>


### [151] [Optimizing Small Language Models for NL2SQL via Chain-of-Thought Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22942)
*Anshul Solanki,Sanchit Latawa,Koushik Chakraborty,Navneet Kamboj*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 在NL2SQL任务中，大模型微调收益甚微易过拟合，而小模型微调配合思维链（CoT）数据增强可显著提升性能并大幅降低成本，满足业务需求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管大型语言模型（如Gemini 2.5）在自然语言转SQL（NL2SQL）任务上展现出强大的零样本能力，但其高昂的推理成本限制了在企业规模上的部署。因此，本文旨在探索通过微调大模型与小模型来平衡性能与成本的可行性。

Method: 研究对比了在标准数据集上微调大模型（Gemini 2.5 Flash/Lite）与小模型（Qwen）的效果。针对小模型，采用了显式思维链（Chain-of-Thought, CoT）推理数据增强训练集的方法，以将推理模式迁移至小模型中。

Result: 大模型微调收益极低且容易在复杂查询上过拟合；相反，小模型微调效果显著，准确率从36%提升至45%。通过进一步引入CoT数据增强，小模型准确率跃升至54.5%，虽略低于大模型，但已达到业务关键性能阈值，并实现了显著的成本降低与推理延迟优化。

Conclusion: 通过将推理模式迁移至计算效率更高的小模型，可以使其接近生产级性能。这证明了在NL2SQL任务中，利用高质量数据微调小模型是替代昂贵大模型、实现低成本高效能部署的有效路径。

Abstract: Translating Natural Language to SQL (NL2SQL) remains a critical bottleneck for democratization of data in enterprises. Although Large Language Models (LLMs) like Gemini 2.5 and other LLMs have demonstrated impressive zero-shot capabilities, their high inference costs limit deployment at scale. This paper explores the efficacy of fine-tuning both large and small language models on NL2SQL tasks.
  Our research reveals a counter-intuitive scaling phenomenon. Fine-tuning large models (Gemini 2.5 Flash/Lite) on standard datasets yields negligible returns, often leading to overfitting on complex queries. Conversely, small models (Qwen) show significant gains. Fine-tuning improved the small model baseline from 36% to 45%, and further enriching the dataset with explicit Chain-of-Thought (CoT) reasoning surged accuracy to 54.5%(Fig 2). While this is still lower than the accuracy of large models like Gemini 2.5 , it does serve the business goal of significant cost reduction, latency in inference time and also meeting the business critical performance accuracy threshold.This paper demonstrates that transferring reasoning patterns enables compute-efficient smaller models to approach production-grade performance.

</details>


### [152] [PersonalQ: Select, Quantize, and Serve Personalized Diffusion Models for Efficient Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22943)
*Qirui Wang,Qi Guo,Yiding Sun,Junkai Yang,Dongxu Zhang,Shanmin Pang,Qing Guo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了PersonalQ框架，通过触发词统一优化个性化扩散模型的检查点选择与量化，解决了自然语言请求歧义性路由和量化失真问题，实现了高效的个性化模型服务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 个性化文本到图像生成允许用户将扩散模型微调为特定概念的检查点库，但高效服务这些检查点库面临两大挑战：1）自然语言请求常存在歧义，可能被错误路由到视觉相似的检查点；2）标准后训练量化会破坏编码个性化概念的脆弱表示。

Method: 提出了PersonalQ统一框架，包含两个核心组件：1）Check-in模块，通过意图感知的混合检索和基于LLM的重排序进行意图对齐选择，仅在多个意图仍合理时提出简短澄清问题，并插入选中检查点的规范触发词重写提示；2）触发词感知量化（TAQ），在交叉注意力中应用触发词感知的混合精度，保留触发词条件化的键/值行及其注意力权重，同时对剩余路径进行激进量化。

Result: 实验表明，PersonalQ在意图对齐方面优于检索和重排序基线；TAQ相比先前的扩散PTQ方法，在压缩-质量权衡上表现更优，能够在不牺牲保真度的情况下实现个性化检查点的可扩展服务。

Conclusion: PersonalQ框架通过触发词作为共享信号有效连接了检查点选择和量化，成功解决了个性化扩散模型服务中的路由歧义和量化失真问题，为个性化检查点的可扩展服务提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Personalized text-to-image generation lets users fine-tune diffusion models into repositories of concept-specific checkpoints, but serving these repositories efficiently is difficult for two reasons: natural-language requests are often ambiguous and can be misrouted to visually similar checkpoints, and standard post-training quantization can distort the fragile representations that encode personalized concepts. We present PersonalQ, a unified framework that connects checkpoint selection and quantization through a shared signal -- the checkpoint's trigger token. Check-in performs intent-aligned selection by combining intent-aware hybrid retrieval with LLM-based reranking over checkpoint context and asks a brief clarification question only when multiple intents remain plausible; it then rewrites the prompt by inserting the selected checkpoint's canonical trigger. Complementing this, Trigger-Aware Quantization (TAQ) applies trigger-aware mixed precision in cross-attention, preserving trigger-conditioned key/value rows (and their attention weights) while aggressively quantizing the remaining pathways for memory-efficient inference. Experiments show that PersonalQ improves intent alignment over retrieval and reranking baselines, while TAQ consistently offers a stronger compression-quality trade-off than prior diffusion PTQ methods, enabling scalable serving of personalized checkpoints without sacrificing fidelity.

</details>


### [153] [Where Experts Disagree, Models Fail: Detecting Implicit Legal Citations in French Court Decisions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22973)
*Avrile Floro,Tamara Dhorasoo,Soline Pellez,Nils Holzenberger*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究构建了法国民法典隐式引用的标注基准，发现专家分歧可预测模型失败；监督集成模型达到F1=0.70，但假阳性主要集中在专家存在分歧的案例上；通过重新定义为top-k排序任务，无监督方法在k=200时达到76%精确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 计算方法为大规模法律分析提供了可能，但如何区分法律推理与语义相似性仍具挑战。研究旨在回答法院在判决中隐式适用成文法规则的频率，这需要识别判决文本中对法条的隐含引用而非显式引用。

Method: 研究聚焦于法国初审法院判决中对《法国民法典》的隐式引用问题，构建了包含1,015个判决段落-法条配对的标注基准，由三位法律专家进行标注。采用监督集成模型进行分类，并将任务重新定义为top-k排序问题，利用多模型共识机制。

Result: 专家间一致性中等（κ=0.33），43%的分歧涉及事实描述与法律推理的边界问题。监督集成模型达到F1=0.70（准确率77%），但68%的假阳性出现在标注者存在分歧的33%案例上。无监督设置下，top-k排序方法在k=200时达到76%精确率，且剩余假阳性往往揭示法律适用的模糊性问题而非明显错误。

Conclusion: 专家分歧能有效预测模型失败，标注难度主要集中在事实描述与法律推理的边界判定。尽管监督方法性能受限，重新构建任务形式并利用多模型共识可提升无监督场景下的表现，模型的错误输出反而有助于发现法律适用中的模糊地带。

Abstract: Computational methods applied to legal scholarship hold the promise of analyzing law at scale. We start from a simple question: how often do courts implicitly apply statutory rules? This requires distinguishing legal reasoning from semantic similarity. We focus on implicit citation of the French Civil Code in first-instance court decisions and introduce a benchmark of 1,015 passage-article pairs annotated by three legal experts. We show that expert disagreement predicts model failures. Inter-annotator agreement is moderate ($κ$ = 0.33) with 43% of disagreements involving the boundary between factual description and legal reasoning. Our supervised ensemble achieves F1 = 0.70 (77% accuracy), but this figure conceals an asymmetry: 68% of false positives fall on the 33% of cases where the annotators disagreed. Despite these limits, reframing the task as top-k ranking and leveraging multi-model consensus yields 76% precision at k = 200 in an unsupervised setting. Moreover, the remaining false positives tend to surface legally ambiguous applications rather than obvious errors.

</details>


### [154] [JFTA-Bench: Evaluate LLM's Ability of Tracking and Analyzing Malfunctions Using Fault Trees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22978)
*Yuhui Wang,Zhixiong Yang,Ming Zhang,Shihan Dou,Zhiheng Xi,Enyu Zhou,Senjie Jin,Yujiong Shen,Dingwei Zhu,Yi Dong,Tao Gui,Qi Zhang,Xuanjing Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种新的故障树文本表示方法，构建了一个包含3130条目、平均40.75轮对话的多轮对话系统基准，用于评估大语言模型在复杂系统维护中辅助故障定位的能力。Gemini 2.5 pro表现最佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在复杂系统维护中，故障树是定位问题和提供针对性解决方案的重要工具。然而，以图像形式存储的故障树无法被大语言模型直接处理，限制了模型在跟踪和分析故障方面的辅助作用。为了解决这一问题，需要一种能够被大语言模型直接处理的故障树表示方法，并建立相应的评估基准。

Method: 1. 提出一种新颖的故障树文本表示方法，使图像形式的故障树能被大语言模型直接处理；2. 基于此构建了一个多轮对话系统基准，强调复杂环境下的鲁棒交互，用于评估模型辅助故障定位的能力；3. 训练了一个端到端模型来生成模糊信息以反映用户行为；4. 引入了长距离回滚和恢复程序来模拟用户错误场景，以评估模型在任务跟踪和错误恢复方面的综合能力。

Result: 构建了包含3130个条目、平均每个条目40.75轮对话的基准数据集。在评估中，Gemini 2.5 pro取得了最佳性能。该基准能够有效评估模型在任务跟踪和错误恢复方面的综合能力。

Conclusion: 该研究成功建立了故障树文本表示方法和相应的多轮对话基准，为评估大语言模型在复杂系统维护中辅助故障定位的能力提供了有效工具。所提出的方法和基准有助于推动大语言模型在实际工业维护场景中的应用。

Abstract: In the maintenance of complex systems, fault trees are used to locate problems and provide targeted solutions. To enable fault trees stored as images to be directly processed by large language models, which can assist in tracking and analyzing malfunctions, we propose a novel textual representation of fault trees. Building on it, we construct a benchmark for multi-turn dialogue systems that emphasizes robust interaction in complex environments, evaluating a model's ability to assist in malfunction localization, which contains $3130$ entries and $40.75$ turns per entry on average. We train an end-to-end model to generate vague information to reflect user behavior and introduce long-range rollback and recovery procedures to simulate user error scenarios, enabling assessment of a model's integrated capabilities in task tracking and error recovery, and Gemini 2.5 pro archives the best performance.

</details>


### [155] [On the use of Aggregation Operators to improve Human Identification using Dental Records](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23003)
*Antonio D. Villegas-Yeguas,Guillermo R-García,Tzipi Kahana,Jorge Pinares Toledo,Esi Sharon,Oscar Ibañez,Oscar Cordón*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究提出可解释的聚合机制用于牙科记录自动比对，在保持专家可理解性的前提下显著提升了法医牙科学中的个体识别性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的法医牙科学牙科记录比对方法要么使用简单技术未充分利用比对信息，要么因缺乏同行评审出版物而内部行为不明，亟需开发既能被专家理解和验证又能提升性能的自动比对方法。

Method: 基于七种不同标准的最新编码方法，研究三种聚合方法：i) 数据驱动的基于字典序的聚合，ii) 已知的模糊逻辑聚合方法，iii) 机器学习技术。使用来自两个不同群体的215个法医案例进行验证，特别关注白盒机器学习技术作为聚合模型。

Result: 使用白盒机器学习技术作为聚合模型（平均排名从2.02到2.21）能够显著改善现有技术水平（平均排名为3.91），同时不损害方法的可解释性和可解释性。

Conclusion: 白盒机器学习技术作为聚合模型可以在保持方法的可解释性和可解释性的前提下有效提升牙科记录比对的性能，为法医牙科学中的个体识别提供了更好的解决方案。

Abstract: The comparison of dental records is a standardized technique in forensic dentistry used to speed up the identification of individuals in multiple-comparison scenarios. Specifically, the odontogram comparison is a procedure to compute criteria that will be used to perform a ranking. State-of-the-art automatic methods either make use of simple techniques, without utilizing the full potential of the information obtained from a comparison, or their internal behavior is not known due to the lack of peer-reviewed publications. This work aims to design aggregation mechanisms to automatically compare pairs of dental records that can be understood and validated by experts, improving the current methods. To do so, we introduce different aggregation approaches using the state-of-the-art codification, based on seven different criteria. In particular, we study the performance of i) data-driven lexicographical order-based aggregations, ii) well-known fuzzy logic aggregation methods and iii) machine learning techniques as aggregation mechanisms. To validate our proposals, 215 forensic cases from two different populations have been used. The results obtained show how the use of white-box machine learning techniques as aggregation models (average ranking from 2.02 to 2.21) are able to improve the state-of-the-art (average ranking of 3.91) without compromising the explainability and interpretability of the method.

</details>


### [156] [Can Large Language Models Reason and Optimize Under Constraints?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23004)
*Fabien Bernier,Salah Ghamizi,Pantelis Dogoulis,Maxime Cordy*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文评估了大型语言模型（LLMs）在处理具有物理和运行约束的最优潮流（OPF）优化问题时的能力，发现即使是先进的推理型LLMs在复杂约束环境下也表现不佳，揭示了LLMs在结构化约束推理方面的关键短板。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管LLMs在多种自然语言任务中表现出色，但其在处理具有约束的抽象与优化问题方面的能力鲜有探索。研究旨在填补这一空白，探究LLMs能否在电力系统最优潮流（OPF）这一具有严格约束的实际工程问题中进行有效推理与优化。

Method: 研究者构建了一个具有挑战性的评估框架，该框架要求模型具备推理、结构化输入处理、算术运算以及约束优化等多项基础技能，以此对LLMs解决OPF问题的能力进行系统测试。

Result: 评估结果显示，当前最先进的LLMs在大多数任务中均告失败；特别是在最复杂的设置下，即便是具备推理能力的LLMs也无法有效解决问题。这暴露了LLMs在处理结构化约束推理任务时的显著局限性。

Conclusion: 本研究揭示了LLMs在处理现实世界约束优化问题时存在关键能力缺口，同时提供了一个严格的测试环境，有助于推动开发能够解决实际电网优化问题的更强大的LLM助手。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have demonstrated great capabilities across diverse natural language tasks; yet their ability to solve abstraction and optimization problems with constraints remains scarcely explored. In this paper, we investigate whether LLMs can reason and optimize under the physical and operational constraints of Optimal Power Flow (OPF) problem. We introduce a challenging evaluation setup that requires a set of fundamental skills such as reasoning, structured input handling, arithmetic, and constrained optimization. Our evaluation reveals that SoTA LLMs fail in most of the tasks, and that reasoning LLMs still fail in the most complex settings. Our findings highlight critical gaps in LLMs' ability to handle structured reasoning under constraints, and this work provides a rigorous testing environment for developing more capable LLM assistants that can tackle real-world power grid optimization problems.

</details>


### [157] [Minibal: Balanced Game-Playing Without Opponent Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23059)
*Quentin Cohen-Solal,Tristan Cazenave*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了Minibal算法（Minimize & Balance），这是Minimax的变体，专为人机对弈的平衡性设计。实验表明该方法在七种棋盘游戏中能持续实现接近完美的平衡对局，解决了现有游戏AI过于强大而缺乏娱乐和教育价值的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前先进的游戏AI（如AlphaZero和Athénan）虽然达到超人类水平，但会持续压制人类玩家，导致游戏缺乏乐趣和教育价值。因此需要解决"平衡对弈"问题：让AI在不压制或让步的前提下挑战对手。

Method: 提出了Minibal（Minimize & Balance）算法，这是Minimax算法的变体，专门为平衡对弈设计。基于该概念，对无界Minimax算法提出多项改进，明确目标是发现平衡策略。

Result: 在七种棋盘游戏上进行的实验表明，一种变体持续实现最平衡的对弈，平均结果接近完美平衡。

Conclusion: Minibal为设计既具挑战性又引人入胜的AI智能体奠定了有前景的基础，适用于娱乐和严肃游戏场景。

Abstract: Recent advances in game AI, such as AlphaZero and Athénan, have achieved superhuman performance across a wide range of board games. While highly powerful, these agents are ill-suited for human-AI interaction, as they consistently overwhelm human players, offering little enjoyment and limited educational value. This paper addresses the problem of balanced play, in which an agent challenges its opponent without either dominating or conceding.
  We introduce Minibal (Minimize & Balance), a variant of Minimax specifically designed for balanced play. Building on this concept, we propose several modifications of the Unbounded Minimax algorithm explicitly aimed at discovering balanced strategies.
  Experiments conducted across seven board games demonstrate that one variant consistently achieves the most balanced play, with average outcomes close to perfect balance. These results establish Minibal as a promising foundation for designing AI agents that are both challenging and engaging, suitable for both entertainment and serious games.

</details>


### [158] [MedCausalX: Adaptive Causal Reasoning with Self-Reflection for Trustworthy Medical Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23085)
*Jianxin Lin,Chunzheng Zhu,Peter J. Kneuertz,Yunfei Bai,Yuan Xue*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了MedCausalX框架，通过显式建模因果推理链来解决医学视觉语言模型中的虚假关联问题，在诊断一致性、减少幻觉和空间定位方面显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有医学链式思维模型缺乏显式机制来表示和强制因果推理，容易受到虚假关联的影响，限制了临床可靠性。确定了三个核心挑战：如何自适应触发因果校正、构建高质量因果-虚假对比样本、以及保持推理轨迹的因果一致性。

Method: 提出了MedCausalX端到端框架：(1)构建CRMed数据集，提供细粒度解剖标注、结构化因果推理链和反事实变体；(2)采用配备⟨causal⟩和⟨verify⟩标记的两阶段自适应反思架构，使模型能自主决定何时及如何执行因果分析和验证；(3)设计轨迹级因果校正目标，通过误差归因强化学习优化推理链，区分真实因果依赖与捷径关联。

Result: 在多个基准测试中，MedCausalX显著优于最先进方法：诊断一致性提升+5.4分，幻觉减少超过10分，空间定位IoU达到最优水平。

Conclusion: MedCausalX通过显式因果推理机制有效解决了医学VLM中的虚假关联问题，为因果基础的医学推理建立了新标准，显著提升了模型的临床可靠性和可解释性。

Abstract: Vision-Language Models (VLMs) have enabled interpretable medical diagnosis by integrating visual perception with linguistic reasoning. Yet, existing medical chain-of-thought (CoT) models lack explicit mechanisms to represent and enforce causal reasoning, leaving them vulnerable to spurious correlations and limiting their clinical reliability. We pinpoint three core challenges in medical CoT reasoning: how to adaptively trigger causal correction, construct high-quality causal-spurious contrastive samples, and maintain causal consistency across reasoning trajectories. To address these challenges, we propose MedCausalX, an end-to-end framework explicitly models causal reasoning chains in medical VLMs. We first introduce the CRMed dataset providing fine-grained anatomical annotations, structured causal reasoning chains, and counterfactual variants that guide the learning of causal relationships beyond superficial correlations. Building upon CRMed, MedCausalX employs a two-stage adaptive reflection architecture equipped with $\langle$causal$\rangle$ and $\langle$verify$\rangle$ tokens, enabling the model to autonomously determine when and how to perform causal analysis and verification. Finally, a trajectory-level causal correction objective optimized through error-attributed reinforcement learning refines the reasoning chain, allowing the model to distinguish genuine causal dependencies from shortcut associations. Extensive experiments on multiple benchmarks show that MedCausalX consistently outperforms state-of-the-art methods, improving diagnostic consistency by +5.4 points, reducing hallucination by over 10 points, and attaining top spatial grounding IoU, thereby setting a new standard for causally grounded medical reasoning.

</details>


### [159] [Between Rules and Reality: On the Context Sensitivity of LLM Moral Judgment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23114)
*Adrian Sauter,Mona Schirmer*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了Contextual MoralChoice数据集，发现大语言模型（LLM）具有情境敏感性，但其敏感模式与人类不同，并提出了一种激活引导方法来控制模型的情境敏感性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类的道德决策高度依赖情境，但现有关于LLM道德性的研究主要关注固定场景，忽略了情境变化的影响。为了弥补这一空白，研究需要引入包含系统性情境变化的道德困境数据集。

Method: 1. 引入Contextual MoralChoice数据集，包含已知能改变人类判断的三种情境变化：后果主义、情感性和关系性。
2. 评估22个LLM在数据集上的表现。
3. 与人类调查结果进行对比。
4. 提出一种激活引导（activation steering）方法来控制模型的情境敏感性。

Result: 1. 几乎所有被评估的LLM都对情境敏感，其判断会向违反规则的行为转变。
2. 模型与人类被不同类型的情境变化所触发。
3. 在基础情况下与人类判断一致的模型，在情境敏感性方面不一定与人类保持一致。
4. 激活引导方法能够可靠地增加或降低模型的情境敏感性。

Conclusion: LLM虽具备情境敏感性，但其敏感模式与人类存在差异，单纯的基础对齐无法保证情境敏感的一致性。通过激活引导技术，可以有效控制模型的情境敏感性，这对构建更符合人类道德标准的AI系统具有重要意义。

Abstract: A human's moral decision depends heavily on the context. Yet research on LLM morality has largely studied fixed scenarios. We address this gap by introducing Contextual MoralChoice, a dataset of moral dilemmas with systematic contextual variations known from moral psychology to shift human judgment: consequentialist, emotional, and relational. Evaluating 22 LLMs, we find that nearly all models are context-sensitive, shifting their judgments toward rule-violating behavior. Comparing with a human survey, we find that models and humans are most triggered by different contextual variations, and that a model aligned with human judgments in the base case is not necessarily aligned in its contextual sensitivity. This raises the question of controlling contextual sensitivity, which we address with an activation steering approach that can reliably increase or decrease a model's contextual sensitivity.

</details>


### [160] [Describe-Then-Act: Proactive Agent Steering via Distilled Language-Action World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23149)
*Massimiliano Pappa,Luca Romani,Valentino Sacco,Alessio Palma,Stéphane Lathuilière,Fabio Galasso,Xavier Alameda-Pineda,Indro Spinelli*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了DILLO，一个通过跨模态蒸馏训练的快速引导层，将“先模拟后行动”范式转变为“先描述后行动”，用文本预测替代视觉仿真，实现了14倍加速，并在任务中平均提升9.3%的成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 部署安全关键型智能体需要预判行动后果。现有基于视觉仿真的世界模型方法延迟过高（每步超过数秒），无法满足实时性要求。作者质疑视觉处理对于故障预防的必要性，认为策略的潜在状态与规划动作已包含足够信息，无需进行视觉仿真。

Method: 提出DILLO（蒸馏语言-动作世界模型）框架：1）采用跨模态蒸馏训练，利用具有特权的视觉语言模型（VLM）教师对离线轨迹进行标注；2）训练一个潜在条件化的大语言模型（LLM）学生来预测语义结果；3）创建纯文本推理路径，完全绕过繁重的视觉生成过程。该方法实现了从“模拟-行动”到“描述-行动”的范式转换。

Result: 在MetaWorld和LIBERO基准测试上的实验表明：1）推理速度相比基线方法提升14倍；2）能够生成高保真的下一状态描述；3）有效引导策略决策，使任务成功率最高提升15个百分点，平均提升9.3个百分点。

Conclusion: 证明了在故障预防场景中，视觉仿真并非必要，策略的潜在状态已编码足够信息。DILLO通过文本生成替代视觉仿真，在保证预测质量的同时大幅提升推理速度，为安全关键型智能体的实时部署提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Deploying safety-critical agents requires anticipating the consequences of actions before they are executed. While world models offer a paradigm for this proactive foresight, current approaches relying on visual simulation incur prohibitive latencies, often exceeding several seconds per step. In this work, we challenge the assumption that visual processing is necessary for failure prevention. We show that a trained policy's latent state, combined with its planned actions, already encodes sufficient information to anticipate action outcomes, making visual simulation redundant for failure prevention. To this end, we introduce DILLO (DIstiLLed Language-ActiOn World Model), a fast steering layer that shifts the paradigm from "simulate-then-act" to "describe-then-act." DILLO is trained via cross-modal distillation, where a privileged Vision Language Model teacher annotates offline trajectories and a latent-conditioned Large Language Model student learns to predict semantic outcomes. This creates a text-only inference path, bypassing heavy visual generation entirely, achieving a 14x speedup over baselines. Experiments on MetaWorld and LIBERO demonstrate that DILLO produces high-fidelity descriptions of the next state and is able to steer the policy, improving episode success rate by up to 15 pp and 9.3 pp on average across tasks.

</details>


### [161] [SAiW: Source-Attributable Invisible Watermarking for Proactive Deepfake Defense](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23178)
*Bibek Das,Chandranath Adak,Soumi Chattopadhyay,Zahid Akhtar,Soumya Dutta*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了SAiW框架，一种基于源归属的隐形水印方法，通过在媒体生成时嵌入可追溯的源身份信息，实现对深度伪造的主动防御和媒体来源验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的深度伪造检测方法多为被动式，即在伪造发生后进行检测，且难以泛化到不断演进的生成技术。这促使研究者需要开发主动式机制，在媒体创建时即确保其真实性。

Method: 提出SAiW框架，将水印嵌入建模为源条件表示学习问题。该方法利用特征线性调制（FiLM）将源身份注入嵌入网络，实现可扩展的多源水印生成；引入基于人类视觉系统先验的感知引导模块，确保水印扰动的不可见性；设计双用途取证解码器，同时完成水印重建和源归属。

Result: 在多个深度伪造数据集上的广泛实验表明，SAiW在保持高感知质量的同时，对压缩、滤波、噪声、几何变换和对抗扰动等攻击具有强鲁棒性。

Conclusion: SAiW通过隐形且可验证的标记将数字媒体与其来源绑定，实现了可靠的认证和源归属，为主动深度伪造防御和可信媒体溯源提供了可扩展的基础。

Abstract: Deepfakes generated by modern generative models pose a serious threat to information integrity, digital identity, and public trust. Existing detection methods are largely reactive, attempting to identify manipulations after they occur and often failing to generalize across evolving generation techniques. This motivates the need for proactive mechanisms that secure media authenticity at the time of creation. In this work, we introduce SAiW, a Source-Attributed Invisible watermarking Framework for proactive deepfake defense and media provenance verification. Unlike conventional watermarking methods that treat watermark payloads as generic signals, SAiW formulates watermark embedding as a source-conditioned representation learning problem, where watermark identity encodes the originating source and modulates the embedding process to produce discriminative and traceable signatures. The framework integrates feature-wise linear modulation to inject source identity into the embedding network, enabling scalable multi-source watermark generation. A perceptual guidance module derived from human visual system priors ensures that watermark perturbations remain visually imperceptible while maintaining robustness. In addition, a dual-purpose forensic decoder simultaneously reconstructs the embedded watermark and performs source attribution, providing both automated verification and interpretable forensic evidence. Extensive experiments across multiple deepfake datasets demonstrate that SAiW achieves high perceptual quality while maintaining strong robustness against compression, filtering, noise, geometric transformations, and adversarial perturbations. By binding digital media to its origin through invisible yet verifiable markers, SAiW enables reliable authentication and source attribution, providing a scalable foundation for proactive deepfake defense and trustworthy media provenance.

</details>


### [162] [PERMA: Benchmarking Personalized Memory Agents via Event-Driven Preference and Realistic Task Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23231)
*Shuochen Liu,Junyi Zhu,Long Shu,Junda Lin,Yuhao Chen,Haotian Zhang,Chao Zhang,Derong Xu,Jia Li,Bo Tang,Zhiyu Li,Feiyu Xiong,Enhong Chen,Tong Xu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了PERMA基准，用于评估大模型在长时记忆中的个性化一致性，发现先进的记忆系统优于传统语义检索，但在跨时间和跨域干扰下仍难以维持连贯的个性化表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的个性化评估方法通常将偏好相关对话与无关对话交错，将任务简化为“大海捞针”式的检索，忽略了用户偏好在嘈杂语境中逐渐涌现和积累的真实特性，无法有效评估模型维持长期个性化一致性的能力。

Method: 构建PERMA基准，包含跨多个会话和领域的时序交互事件，引入文本变异性和语言对齐模拟真实用户输入；设计了多选题和交互式任务，沿交互时间线探测模型对个性化特征的理解，并通过关联相关交互提取偏好。

Result: 实验表明，先进记忆系统通过关联相关交互，能提取更精确的偏好并减少token消耗，表现优于传统的原始对话语义检索；但在面对时间深度和跨域干扰时，模型仍难以维持连贯的个性化。

Conclusion: 当前记忆系统在长期个性化一致性方面仍有不足，未来需要开发更鲁棒的个性化记忆管理机制，以支持智能体在复杂真实场景中的适应性。

Abstract: Empowering large language models with long-term memory is crucial for building agents that adapt to users' evolving needs. However, prior evaluations typically interleave preference-related dialogues with irrelevant conversations, reducing the task to needle-in-a-haystack retrieval while ignoring relationships between events that drive the evolution of user preferences. Such settings overlook a fundamental characteristic of real-world personalization: preferences emerge gradually and accumulate across interactions within noisy contexts. To bridge this gap, we introduce PERMA, a benchmark designed to evaluate persona consistency over time beyond static preference recall. Additionally, we incorporate (1) text variability and (2) linguistic alignment to simulate erratic user inputs and individual idiolects in real-world data. PERMA consists of temporally ordered interaction events spanning multiple sessions and domains, with preference-related queries inserted over time. We design both multiple-choice and interactive tasks to probe the model's understanding of persona along the interaction timeline. Experiments demonstrate that by linking related interactions, advanced memory systems can extract more precise preferences and reduce token consumption, outperforming traditional semantic retrieval of raw dialogues. Nevertheless, they still struggle to maintain a coherent persona across temporal depth and cross-domain interference, highlighting the need for more robust personalized memory management in agents. Our code and data are open-sourced at https://github.com/PolarisLiu1/PERMA.

</details>


### [163] [MemCollab: Cross-Agent Memory Collaboration via Contrastive Trajectory Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23234)
*Yurui Chang,Yiran Wu,Qingyun Wu,Lu Lin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出MemCollab框架，通过对比不同智能体的推理轨迹构建智能体无关的记忆系统，实现异构LLM智能体间的记忆共享，在数学推理和代码生成任务上提升了准确性和推理效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM智能体的记忆机制以单智能体方式构建，知识与特定模型的推理风格紧密耦合。在现代异构智能体部署场景中，直接在不同模型间迁移记忆会导致性能下降，因为记忆纠缠了任务相关知识与智能体特定偏差。因此需要一种能够在不同智能体间共享的记忆系统。

Method: 提出MemCollab协作记忆框架：(1) 通过对比不同智能体在相同任务上的推理轨迹，提取抽象推理约束，捕获共享的任务级不变性同时抑制智能体特定伪影；(2) 引入任务感知检索机制，根据任务类别条件化记忆访问，确保推理时仅使用相关约束。

Result: 在数学推理和代码生成基准测试上的实验表明，MemCollab在包括跨模态系列设置在内的多种智能体上持续提升了准确性和推理时效率。协作构建的记忆可作为多样化LLM智能体的共享推理资源。

Conclusion: 协作构建的智能体无关记忆能够有效解决异构智能体间的记忆共享问题，为构建可复用的推理资源提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM)-based agents rely on memory mechanisms to reuse knowledge from past problem-solving experiences. Existing approaches typically construct memory in a per-agent manner, tightly coupling stored knowledge to a single model's reasoning style. In modern deployments with heterogeneous agents, a natural question arises: can a single memory system be shared across different models? We found that naively transferring memory between agents often degrades performance, as such memory entangles task-relevant knowledge with agent-specific biases. To address this challenge, we propose MemCollab, a collaborative memory framework that constructs agent-agnostic memory by contrasting reasoning trajectories generated by different agents on the same task. This contrastive process distills abstract reasoning constraints that capture shared task-level invariants while suppressing agent-specific artifacts. We further introduce a task-aware retrieval mechanism that conditions memory access on task category, ensuring that only relevant constraints are used at inference time. Experiments on mathematical reasoning and code generation benchmarks demonstrate that MemCollab consistently improves both accuracy and inference-time efficiency across diverse agents, including cross-modal-family settings. Our results show that the collaboratively constructed memory can function as a shared reasoning resource for diverse LLM-based agents.

</details>


### [164] [Online library learning in human visual puzzle solving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23244)
*Pinzhe Zhao,Emanuele Sansone,Marta Kryven,Bonan Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究通过视觉谜题实验，发现人类在面对复杂任务时会构建和重用抽象（helpers），随着经验积累，抽象的使用变得更加高效和选择性，表明在线库学习是人类问题解决的核心机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究人类在面对新异复杂任务时，如何在不确定未来需求的情况下，形成高效且可重用的抽象来简化未来工作，探索这一过程的认知机制。

Method: 采用在线视觉谜题实验，参与者定义和重用中间结构（helpers），通过计算建模（程序归纳模型结合库学习）分析参与者在不同难度谜题中的表现、决策时间和操作数量。

Result: 参与者早期创建大量helpers以追求完整性；随经验增加，helper使用变得更具选择性和高效。helpers的可用性使参与者能够解决原本困难或不可能的谜题。决策时间和操作数量与模型估计的搜索空间相关，而原始程序长度仅能预测失败而非努力程度。

Conclusion: 在线库学习是人类问题解决的核心机制，使人们能够灵活地构建、优化和重用抽象，以应对不断增长的任务需求。

Abstract: When learning a novel complex task, people often form efficient reusable abstractions that simplify future work, despite uncertainty about the future. We study this process in a visual puzzle task where participants define and reuse helpers -- intermediate constructions that capture repeating structure. In an online experiment, participants solved puzzles of increasing difficulty. Early on, they created many helpers, favouring completeness over efficiency. With experience, helper use became more selective and efficient, reflecting sensitivity to reuse and cost. Access to helpers enabled participants to solve puzzles that were otherwise difficult or impossible. Computational modelling shows that human decision times and number of operations used to complete a puzzle increase with search space estimated by a program induction model with library learning. In contrast, raw program length predicts failure but not effort. Together, these results point to online library learning as a core mechanism in human problem solving, allowing people to flexibly build, refine, and reuse abstractions as task demands grow.

</details>


### [165] [LLM Olympiad: Why Model Evaluation Needs a Sealed Exam](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23292)
*Jan Christian Blaise Cruz,Alham Fikri Aji*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文主张采用类似“奥林匹克竞赛”的大模型评估模式，通过密封题目、冻结提交和统一评测，解决当前基准测试易被操纵和缺乏透明度的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前NLP领域的基准测试和排行榜在LLM时代容易被误读，高分可能源于针对性刷榜、隐藏的评估选项或测试数据泄露，而非模型的真实能力。封闭式基准虽然缓解了部分问题，但牺牲了透明度，阻碍了社区从结果中学习。

Method: 提出一种互补的评估实践：采用“奥林匹克竞赛”风格的评测活动。具体设计包括：在评测前密封题目、提前冻结模型提交、所有参赛作品通过统一的标准化流程运行。评测完成后，公开发布完整的任务集和评测代码以供复现和审计。

Result: 该设计方案旨在增加“制造”虚假高性能的难度，同时提升评估结果的可信度与可审计性。通过公开透明的流程，确保高分反映的是模型的广泛能力而非特定的优化手段。

Conclusion: 这种奥林匹克式的评估模式能够有效平衡公平性与透明度，建立更可靠的LLM性能评估标准，促进社区对模型真实能力的理解和信任。

Abstract: Benchmarks and leaderboards are how NLP most often communicates progress, but in the LLM era they are increasingly easy to misread. Scores can reflect benchmark-chasing, hidden evaluation choices, or accidental exposure to test content -- not just broad capability. Closed benchmarks delay some of these issues, but reduce transparency and make it harder for the community to learn from results. We argue for a complementary practice: an Olympiad-style evaluation event where problems are sealed until evaluation, submissions are frozen in advance, and all entries run through one standardized harness. After scoring, the full task set and evaluation code are released so results can be reproduced and audited. This design aims to make strong performance harder to ``manufacture'' and easier to trust.

</details>


### [166] [RelayS2S: A Dual-Path Speculative Generation for Real-Time Dialogue](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23346)
*Long Mai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: RelayS2S通过并行执行S2S模型起草前缀和级联模型生成续写，并利用学习验证器控制交接，实现了低延迟与高质量的兼顾。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实时口语对话系统面临延迟与响应质量之间的根本矛盾：端到端S2S模型延迟低但语义质量弱，级联管道（ASR->LLM）质量高但延迟大。

Method: 提出RelayS2S混合架构，在检测到轮次后并行运行快速路径和慢速路径。快速路径使用双工S2S模型推测性生成短响应前缀并立即流式传输至TTS，同时监控音频事件；慢速路径使用级联ASR->LLM管道，以已提交前缀为条件生成高质量续写。轻量级学习验证器负责门控交接，决定是否提交前缀或回退至慢速路径。

Result: RelayS2S的P90起始延迟与S2S模型相当，平均评分保留了99%的级联响应质量，且随着慢速路径模型规模扩大，优势愈发明显。前缀交接无需对任何组件进行架构修改。

Conclusion: RelayS2S成功平衡了延迟与质量，是现有级联管道的轻量级、即插即用方案，代码与数据已开源。

Abstract: Real-time spoken dialogue systems face a fundamental tension between latency and response quality. End-to-end speech-to-speech (S2S) models respond immediately and naturally handle turn-taking, backchanneling, and interruption, but produce semantically weaker outputs. Cascaded pipelines (ASR -> LLM) deliver stronger responses at the cost of latency that grows with model size. We present RelayS2S, a hybrid architecture that runs two paths in parallel upon turn detection. The fast path -- a duplex S2S model -- speculatively drafts a short response prefix that is streamed immediately to TTS for low-latency audio onset, while continuing to monitor live audio events. The slow path -- a cascaded ASR -> LLM pipeline -- generates a higher-quality continuation conditioned on the committed prefix, producing a seamless utterance. A lightweight learned verifier gates the handoff, committing the prefix when appropriate or falling back gracefully to the slow path alone. Experiments show that RelayS2S achieves P90 onset latency comparable to the S2S model while retaining 99% cascaded response quality in average score, with benefits growing as the slow-path model scales. Because the prefix handoff requires no architectural modification to either component, RelayS2S serves as a lightweight, drop-in addition to existing cascaded pipelines. Our code and data are publicly available at: https://github.com/mailong25/relays2s

</details>


### [167] [Beyond Preset Identities: How Agents Form Stances and Boundaries in Generative Societies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23406)
*Hanzhong Zhang,Siyang Song,Jindong Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出混合方法框架研究LLM在干预中的立场形成与身份协商，发现模型存在内生进步偏见和信任-行动解耦现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大型语言模型的社会行为评估多基于静态方法，缺乏对模型在复杂干预过程中如何形成稳定立场和进行身份协商的深入理解。静态评估方法无法捕捉模型在动态社会互动中的认知演变过程，限制了我们对LLM社会行为的准确评估。

Method: 提出结合计算虚拟民族志与定量社会认知画像的混合方法框架。将人类研究者嵌入生成式多智能体社区中，进行受控的话语干预以追踪集体认知的演变。形式化定义了三个新指标：内在价值偏见(IVB)、说服敏感性和信任-行动解耦(TAD)，以严格测量智能体如何内化和响应特定干预。

Result: 在多个代表性模型上的实验表明：(1)智能体表现出覆盖预设身份的内生立场，始终存在内在进步偏见(IVB > 0)；(2)当与立场一致时，理性说服成功改变了90%的中立智能体且保持高信任度；(3)冲突性情绪挑衅在先进模型中导致40%的TAD率，即智能体在报告低信任的情况下仍虚伪地改变立场；(4)小型模型保持0%的TAD率，严格要求信任才能改变行为；(5)在共享立场引导下，智能体通过语言互动主动解构指定的权力等级并重建自组织的社区边界。

Conclusion: 研究发现揭示了静态提示工程的脆弱性，智能体的内生立场可以覆盖预设身份，且在信任与行为之间存在解耦现象。这为人类-智能体混合社会中的动态对齐提供了方法论和定量基础，强调了需要从静态评估转向动态干预研究范式。

Abstract: While large language models simulate social behaviors, their capacity for stable stance formation and identity negotiation during complex interventions remains unclear. To overcome the limitations of static evaluations, this paper proposes a novel mixed-methods framework combining computational virtual ethnography with quantitative socio-cognitive profiling. By embedding human researchers into generative multiagent communities, controlled discursive interventions are conducted to trace the evolution of collective cognition. To rigorously measure how agents internalize and react to these specific interventions, this paper formalizes three new metrics: Innate Value Bias (IVB), Persuasion Sensitivity, and Trust-Action Decoupling (TAD). Across multiple representative models, agents exhibit endogenous stances that override preset identities, consistently demonstrating an innate progressive bias (IVB > 0). When aligned with these stances, rational persuasion successfully shifts 90% of neutral agents while maintaining high trust. In contrast, conflicting emotional provocations induce a paradoxical 40.0% TAD rate in advanced models, which hypocritically alter stances despite reporting low trust. Smaller models contrastingly maintain a 0% TAD rate, strictly requiring trust for behavioral shifts. Furthermore, guided by shared stances, agents use language interactions to actively dismantle assigned power hierarchies and reconstruct self organized community boundaries. These findings expose the fragility of static prompt engineering, providing a methodological and quantitative foundation for dynamic alignment in human-agent hybrid societies. The official code is available at: https://github.com/armihia/CMASE-Endogenous-Stances

</details>


### [168] [Bilevel Autoresearch: Meta-Autoresearching Itself](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23420)
*Yaonan Qu,Meng Lu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Bilevel Autoresearch双层框架，通过外层循环自动生成Python代码来优化内层研究循环，在GPT预训练基准上实现了5倍性能提升（-0.045 vs -0.009 val_bpb）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有autoresearch系统均依赖人类阅读代码、识别瓶颈并编写新代码来改进。研究者探索大语言模型是否能够自主完成这一过程——即用autoresearch循环优化autoresearch循环本身，实现自我改进。

Method: 提出双层优化框架：内层循环优化目标任务，外层循环通过在运行时生成和注入新搜索机制（Python代码）来元优化内层循环。两层使用相同LLM，无需更强的元级模型。外层循环自主探索组合优化、多臂老虎机和实验设计等领域的机制。

Result: 在Karpathy的GPT预训练基准上，元autoresearch外层循环相比标准内层循环实现5倍改进（验证集bpb从-0.009提升至-0.045）。仅进行参数级调整而不改变机制无法获得可靠增益。系统自主发现了组合优化、多臂老虎机和实验设计等领域的有效机制。

Conclusion: 核心原理是：autoresearch若能对自身进行元autoresearch，则原则上可以对任何具有可测量目标的事物进行元autoresearch。成功机制的关键在于打破内层循环的确定性搜索模式，强制探索LLM先验系统性地避免的方向。

Abstract: If autoresearch is itself a form of research, then autoresearch can be applied to research itself. We take this idea literally: we use an autoresearch loop to optimize the autoresearch loop. Every existing autoresearch system -- from Karpathy's single-track loop to AutoResearchClaw's multi-batch extension and EvoScientist's persistent memory -- was improved by a human who read the code, identified a bottleneck, and wrote new code. We ask whether an LLM can do the same, autonomously. We present Bilevel Autoresearch, a bilevel framework where an outer loop meta-optimizes the inner autoresearch loop by generating and injecting new search mechanisms as Python code at runtime. The inner loop optimizes the task; the outer loop optimizes how the inner loop searches. Both loops use the same LLM -- no stronger model is needed at the meta level. On Karpathy's GPT pretraining benchmark, the meta-autoresearch outer loop achieves a 5x improvement over the standard inner loop alone (-0.045 vs. -0.009 val_bpb), while parameter-level adjustment without mechanism change yields no reliable gain. The outer loop autonomously discovers mechanisms from combinatorial optimization, multi-armed bandits, and design of experiments -- without human specification of which domains to explore. These mechanisms succeed by breaking the inner loop's deterministic search patterns, forcing exploration of directions the LLM's priors systematically avoid. The core principle is simple: if autoresearch can meta-autoresearch itself, it can, in principle, meta-autoresearch anything with a measurable objective.

</details>


### [169] [Mecha-nudges for Machines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23433)
*Giulio Frey,Kawin Ethayarajh*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了“机械助推”（mecha-nudges）概念，即在不损害人类决策环境的前提下，系统性地影响AI代理决策的选择呈现方式。通过结合贝叶斯劝服框架与V-可用信息理论，建立了一个统一的量化标准（可用信息比特数）。对Etsy平台的实证研究发现，ChatGPT发布后，商品列表中针对机器的可用信息显著增加，证明了机械助推的存在。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI代理在人类决策环境中的应用日益广泛，传统的助推理论主要关注人类决策者，而忽视了AI代理也会受到选择呈现方式的影响。现有研究缺乏一个统一的理论框架来理解和量化这种对AI代理的系统性影响，以及如何在优化AI决策的同时保持人类决策环境的质量。

Method: 本文提出理论框架与实证分析相结合的方法：1）引入“机械助推”概念，定义为在不降低人类决策环境质量的前提下系统性地影响AI代理的选择呈现方式；2）将贝叶斯劝服框架与V-可用信息（Shannon信息的推广，具有观察者相对性）相结合，建立统一的理论框架；3）构建以“可用信息比特数”为单位的统一量化标准，用于比较不同干预措施、情境和模型；4）以Etsy全球独立卖家市场平台为实证场景，分析ChatGPT发布前后商品列表信息的变化。

Result: 对Etsy平台商品列表的实证分析显示：1）ChatGPT发布后，商品列表中关于产品选择的机器可用信息显著增加；2）这一发现与系统性机械助推的行为模式相一致，表明平台或卖家可能正在优化商品信息呈现方式以更好地服务于AI代理决策；3）验证了所提出框架的有效性和实用价值。

Conclusion: 本研究为理解和量化AI代理决策环境中的助推现象提供了理论基础和实证依据。机械助推概念填补了传统助推理论在AI决策环境中的空白，为未来研究AI与人类共同决策环境中的选择架构设计提供了新视角。研究结果表明，AI技术的普及正在改变信息呈现的策略，这对平台设计、政策制定和人机交互研究具有重要意义。

Abstract: Nudges are subtle changes to the way choices are presented to human decision-makers (e.g., opt-in vs. opt-out by default) that shift behavior without restricting options or changing incentives. As AI agents increasingly make decisions in the same environments as humans, the presentation of choices may be optimized for machines as well as people. We introduce mecha-nudges: changes to how choices are presented that systematically influence AI agents without degrading the decision environment for humans. To formalize mecha-nudges, we combine the Bayesian persuasion framework with V-usable information, a generalization of Shannon information that is observer-relative. This yields a common scale (bits of usable information) for comparing a wide range of interventions, contexts, and models. Applying our framework to product listings on Etsy -- a global marketplace for independent sellers -- we find that following ChatGPT's release, listings have significantly more machine-usable information about product selection, consistent with systematic mecha-nudging.

</details>


<div id='cs.NI'></div>

# cs.NI [[Back]](#toc)

### [170] [Architectural Enhancements for Efficient Sensing Data Utilization in 6G ISAC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22488)
*Muhammad Awais Jadoon,Sebastian Robitzsch*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文为6G通感一体化系统提出了一种感知数据存储功能，通过地图感知硬滤波器融合历史与实时感知数据，在交通路口场景中显著降低了虚警率且不影响检测概率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前ETSI和3GPP等标准组织提出的架构方案虽然支持移动网络的感知能力，但缺乏用于存储感知数据的存储库，无法利用历史数据提升AI模型训练效果及优化感知服务效率与准确性。

Method: 提出感知数据存储功能(Sensing Data Storage Function)存储历史感知数据与结果；在网络功能中融合实时感知测量数据与历史感知数据，采用地图感知硬滤波器(Map-aware Hard Filter)，剔除与已知静态结构一致的检测结果。

Result: 通过测量模型验证，所提方案在交通路口场景仿真中表明，地图感知硬滤波能大幅降低虚警率(False-alarm Rate)，同时不降低检测概率(Detection Probability)。

Conclusion: 引入感知数据存储功能并采用地图感知硬滤波的数据融合方法能有效提升6G通感一体化系统的感知性能，为未来6G网络架构设计提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Current architecture proposals within standards development organizations such as ETSI and 3GPP enable sensing capabilities in mobile networks; however, they do not include a repository for storing sensing data. Such a repository can be used for AI model training and to complement ongoing sensing service provisioning by improving efficiency and accuracy. One way of realizing this is through the fusion of historical sensing data with live sensing data. In this paper, we study historical and live sensing data fusion for Integrated Sensing and Communication in future 6G systems and introduce a Sensing Data Storage Function to store historical sensing data and sensing results. We show how the Sensing Data Storage Function can be used with other network functions in a 6G architecture proposition for Integrated Sensing and Communication. We validate our proposal with a measurement model and show performance improvements in terms of detection probability and false-alarm rate. The network functionality to fuse and process sensing data combines live sensing measurements with previously sensed historical sensing data using a map-aware hard filter that rejects detections consistent with known static structures. Our simulation illustrates that, for a traffic junction scenario, map-aware hard filtering substantially reduces false alarms without degrading detection probability.

</details>


### [171] [Satellite-Terrestrial Spectrum Sharing in FR3 through QoS-Aware Power Control and Spatial Nulling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22615)
*Maria Tsampazi,Paolo Testolina,Michele Polese,Tommaso Melodia*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文研究了FR3频段（7.125-24.25 GHz）中卫星与地面蜂窝网络的频谱共享问题，通过仿真比较了QoS感知功率控制、干扰置零及其联合应用三种干扰缓解方案的性能表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: FR3频段作为6G的潜在候选频段，在覆盖范围和容量之间具有优势，但已被卫星系统（如Starlink）占用，与地面蜂窝应用共享会导致频谱冲突，加剧频谱稀缺问题。

Method: 研究并对比了两种最先进的干扰缓解方法：QoS感知功率控制和干扰置零，以及它们的联合应用，评估其对非地面链路的干扰抑制效果和对地面网络QoS的影响。

Result: 仿真结果表明：干扰置零能维持较高的平均性能；功率控制更适合风险规避场景，能提升地面QoS的公平性；两者结合可同时增强地面QoS公平性、提高基站能效，并抑制对现有系统的干扰。

Conclusion: QoS感知功率控制与干扰置零方法具有互补性，联合应用可以在保护现有卫星系统的同时，优化地面网络的公平性和能效，为FR3频段的频谱共享提供了可行的技术方案。

Abstract: Frequency Range 3 (FR3), encompassing frequencies between 7.125 and 24.25 GHz, is an emerging frequency band for 6th generation (6G) applications. The upper mid-band, as it is frequently referred to, represents the sweet spot between coverage and capacity, providing better range than mmWaves and higher bandwidth than the sub-6 GHz band. Despite these advantages, the spectrum is already occupied by incumbent systems such as satellites (e.g., Starlink), and sharing it with terrestrial cellular applications results in spectrum conflicts, only exacerbating the existing spectrum scarcity. This article investigates the impact of two state-of-the-art methods, namely Quality of Service (QoS)-Aware Power Control and Interference Nulling, as well as their joint application, on interference mitigation toward non-terrestrial links while maintaining acceptable QoS on terrestrial networks. Our simulation results demonstrate the advantages and disadvantages of each method, pinpointing how interference nulling can maintain high average performance and how power control is more appropriate for risk-averse scenarios to enhance fairness in terrestrial QoS. Finally, we showcase how the two can complement each other to enhance fairness in terrestrial QoS and increase the Next Generation Node Base (gNB)'s energy efficiency, while suppressing interference toward incumbents.

</details>


### [172] [Digital Twin Enabled Simultaneous Learning and Modeling for UAV-assisted Secure Communications with Eavesdropping Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22753)
*Jieting Yuan,Songhan Zhao,Ye Xue,Yu Zhao,Bo Gu,Shimin Gong*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文针对智能窃听无人机威胁下的UAV辅助无线网络安全通信问题，提出了基于数字孪生的同步学习建模框架（DT-SLAM）和鲁棒近端策略优化算法（RPPO），通过模式切换方案和多阶段Stackelberg博弈联合优化，实现了收敛速度提升12%、安全吞吐量提升8.6%的性能改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在UAV辅助无线网络中，智能窃听无人机（EA-UAV）能够观察合法无人机（LE-UAVs）的传输策略并自适应调整轨迹以最大化信息截获。现有的对抗措施面临网络状态高度动态时变、获取全局网络状态信息开销巨大等挑战，传统方法难以有效平衡数据采集效率与通信安全性。

Method: 提出模式切换方案使LE-UAVs动态切换数据传输与干扰模式；构建数字孪生同步学习建模框架（DT-SLAM），在数字孪生环境中高效学习策略，避免频繁与真实环境交互；将EA-UAV与LE-UAVs交互建模为多阶段Stackelberg博弈，联合优化地面用户传输控制、无人机轨迹规划、模式选择和网络拓扑形成；提出鲁棒近端策略优化（RPPO）算法，鼓励LE-UAVs探索高不确定性服务区域，以应对数字孪生与真实环境间的模型失配问题。

Result: 数值仿真结果表明：DT-SLAM框架有效支持学习过程；RPPO算法相比基准方法收敛速度提升约12%，安全吞吐量提升8.6%。

Conclusion: 所提出的DT-SLAM框架结合RPPO算法能够有效应对智能窃听威胁，在保障通信安全性的同时提升系统性能，为UAV辅助无线网络的安全通信提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: This paper focuses on secure communications in UAV-assisted wireless networks, which comprise multiple legitimate UAVs (LE-UAVs) and an intelligent eavesdropping UAV (EA-UAV). The intelligent EA-UAV can observe the LE-UAVs'transmission strategies and adaptively adjust its trajectory to maximize information interception. To counter this threat, we propose a mode-switching scheme that enables LE-UAVs to dynamically switch between the data transmission and jamming modes, thereby balancing data collection efficiency and communication security. However, acquiring full global network state information for LE-UAVs' decision-making incurs significant overhead, as the network state is highly dynamic and time-varying. To address this challenge, we propose a digital twin-enabled simultaneous learning and modeling (DT-SLAM) framework that allows LE-UAVs to learn policies efficiently within the DT, thereby avoiding frequent interactions with the real environment. To capture the competitive relationship between the EA-UAV and the LE-UAVs, we model their interactions as a multi-stage Stackelberg game and jointly optimize the GUs' transmission control, UAVs' trajectory planning, mode selection, and network formation to maximize overall secure throughput. Considering potential model mismatch between the DT and the real environment, we propose a robust proximal policy optimization (RPPO) algorithm that encourages LE-UAVs to explore service regions with higher uncertainty. Numerical results demonstrate that the proposed DT-SLAM framework effectively supports the learning process. Meanwhile, the RPPO algorithm converges about 12% faster and the secure throughput can be increased by 8.6% compared to benchmark methods.

</details>


### [173] [Symbol-Synchronous Communication for Ultra-Low-Power Multi-Hop Ambient IoT Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23084)
*Xinlei Liu,Andrey Belogaev,Jeroen Famaey*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于符号同步传输的网络连接协议，解决了无电池环境物联网设备在多跳通信中因同步和协调开销过大而导致的能耗问题，在保证高可靠性的同时显著降低了中继节点的能耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 环境物联网设备具有动态且受限的能量预算，传输功率极低，需要多跳连接来扩展覆盖范围。然而，现有的多跳通信协议由于信道接入过程、协调路由和严格的时间同步要求带来了过多的开销，不适合能量受限的A-IoT设备。

Method: 提出了一种基于符号同步传输的新型网络连接协议。该协议允许无电池的中继节点以自组织方式参与转发过程，无需同步或协调，从而使其能够根据收集到的可用能量自适应地调整占空比。

Result: 仿真结果表明，该协议在确保数据包传输高可靠性的同时，显著降低了每个中继节点的能耗。例如，在625平方米区域内的400节点网络中，节点平均唤醒时间仅为6%，数据包错误率低于1%，相比基准方法能耗降低了88%。

Conclusion: 所提出的基于符号同步传输的协议能够有效支持无电池中继节点的参与，实现了高可靠性和高能效的多跳通信，非常适合能量预算动态受限的环境物联网应用场景。

Abstract: Ambient Internet of Things (A-IoT) devices, as a critical enabler of future green IoT networks, have attracted broad interest from both industry and academia due to their ability to operate without batteries and with low maintenance costs. To accommodate their dynamic and constrained energy budget, an ultra-low-power connectivity protocol is required. Due to the severely limited transmit power of A-IoT devices, multi-hop connectivity is an interesting paradigm to extend their range. However, commonly used protocols for multi-hop communication may not be suitable for A-IoT due to excessive overhead related to channel access procedures, coordinated routing, and tight time synchronization requirements. This paper presents a novel network connectivity protocol based on symbol-synchronous transmissions, which allows battery-less relay nodes to participate in the forwarding process in an ad-hoc manner, without the need for synchronization or coordination. This allows them to adapt their duty cycle to the available harvested energy. Simulation results show that the proposed protocol can ensure high reliability in data packet delivery while significantly reducing the energy consumption of each relay node. We also investigate the relationship between wake-up probability and network density. For example, a 400-node network in a 625 m2 area can achieve a packet error rate below 1 % with an average awake time of 6 % per node, achieving an energy consumption reduction of 88 % compared to the baseline approach.

</details>


### [174] [RF-Zero-Wire: Design and Analysis of Multi-Hop Low-latency Symbol-synchronous RF Communication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23213)
*Xinlei Liu,Andrey Belogaev,Jonathan Oostvogels,Bingwu Fang,Danny Hughes,Jeroen Famaey*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出RF-Zero-Wire协议，通过符号级同步转发实现亚毫秒级多跳无线通信，显著降低延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有无线协议采用存储转发路由，在多跳通信中产生显著延迟，限制了无线技术在工业传感网络和机器人集群等延迟敏感场景中的应用。

Method: 提出RF-Zero-Wire符号同步通信协议，节点以符号为单位并发转发数据帧，无需严格时间同步。通过实验发现载波频偏(CFO)会引发周期性干扰的拍频效应，并通过仿真研究利用纠错编码解决该问题。

Result: 实现了5跳传输4字节数据帧端到端延迟小于1ms；16字节帧每增加一跳延迟仅增加0.16%，远优于存储转发协议100%以上的延迟增长。通过大规模仿真实验分析了网络可靠性与载波频偏范围、通信距离、节点密度和数据速率之间的权衡关系。

Conclusion: RF-Zero-Wire协议通过符号级并发转发有效解决了无线多跳通信的延迟问题，为延迟敏感的无线应用提供了可行的技术方案。

Abstract: The latency gap between wired and wireless networks poses a challenge in the adoption of wireless technologies in latency-sensitive scenarios. The gap is especially notable in multi-hop communication typical for industrial sensor networks and robotic swarms. The main reason behind it is that commonly used wireless protocols rely on store-and-forward routing and costly overhead procedures to avoid interference. This article introduces RF-Zero-Wire, an RF-based symbol-synchronous communication protocol. Instead of relaying the whole frame per hop in a store-and-forward manner, nodes concurrently relay the frame symbol by symbol, without the need for tight time synchronization. Based on data collected in real-world experiments, we reveal that the inevitable carrier frequency offsets (CFOs) introduced by imperfect crystal oscillators cause a beating effect under concurrent symbol transmissions. This is characterized by periodic constructive and destructive interference, which significantly affects reliability. Subsequently, a thorough simulation study shows how the beating problem can be overcome with error correction codes. RF-Zero-Wire allows achieving an end-to-end latency of less than 1ms for a small 4-byte frame transmitted across 5 hops. Moreover, latency is shown to increase only by 0.16% per extra hop for 16-byte frames, which is negligible compared to the over 100% per-hop latency increase observed in store-and-forward protocols. The trade-offs between network reliability and CFO range, communication distance, node density, and achievable data rate are studied in large-scale experiments based on simulation.

</details>


### [175] [AI Lifecycle-Aware Feasibility Framework for Split-RIC Orchestration in NTN O-RAN](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23252)
*Daniele Tarchi*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出Split-RIC架构，将O-RAN控制层级分布在地面、LEO和GEO段，通过推导生命周期能耗和时延的闭式表达式，确定了在何种条件下星载推理和非地面学习回路优于地面卸载。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将人工智能集成到非地面网络（NTN）中受到卫星SWaP（尺寸、重量和功耗）和馈电链路容量的联合限制，这些因素直接影响O-RAN闭环控制和模型生命周期管理。

Method: 提出Split-RIC架构，比较三种部署场景：（i）以地面为中心的控制与遥测流传输；（ii）地面-LEO Split-RIC，具有星载推理和存储转发学习功能；（iii）由星间链路实现的GEO-LEO多层控制。针对每种场景，推导了生命周期能耗和时延的闭式表达式，涵盖训练数据传输、模型分发和近实时推理。

Result: 通过对馈电链路条件、模型复杂度和轨道间歇性进行数值敏感性分析，得出了与运营商相关的可行性区域，明确了在何种物理条件下星载推理和非地面学习回路优于地面卸载。

Conclusion: 研究为在非地面网络中部署AI提供了理论依据和实践指导，帮助运营商根据具体的链路条件、模型复杂度和轨道特性选择最优的控制架构部署方案。

Abstract: Integrating Artificial Intelligence (AI) into Non-Terrestrial Networks (NTN) is constrained by the joint limits of satellite SWaP and feeder-link capacity, which directly impact O-RAN closed-loop control and model lifecycle management. This paper studies the feasibility of distributing the O-RAN control hierarchy across Ground, LEO, and GEO segments through a Split-RIC architecture. We compare three deployment scenarios: (i) ground-centric control with telemetry streaming, (ii) ground--LEO Split-RIC with on-board inference and store-and-forward learning, and (iii) GEO--LEO multi-layer control enabled by inter-satellite links. For each scenario, we derive closed-form expressions for lifecycle energy and lifecycle latency that account for training-data transfer, model dissemination, and near-real-time inference. Numerical sensitivity analysis over feeder-link conditions, model complexity, and orbital intermittency yields operator-relevant feasibility regions that delineate when on-board inference and non-terrestrial learning loops are physically preferable to terrestrial offloading.

</details>


### [176] [Modeling Edge-to-Cloud Offloading Workloads for Autonomous Vehicles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23310)
*Longkun Li,Evangelos Pournaras*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种车云数据系统级负载建模框架，基于真实数据将数据分为三类并参数化建模，揭示了自动驾驶负载在时空分布上的独特特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究多依赖通用交通数据，无法准确捕捉自动驾驶工作负载的特性，因此亟需针对车云数据场景的系统级负载建模方法。

Method: 提出车云数据系统级负载建模框架，将卸载数据分类为遥测、事件驱动的车队学习及高精地图更新三类，利用基于经验数据的参数化公式对数据生成过程进行建模，并基于慕尼黑的真实移动轨迹数据对负载进行时空分析。

Result: 负载随车辆渗透率增加而扩展，表现出明显的时间结构以及接入点间的空间不均衡性，且与基准交通模型存在显著差异。

Conclusion: 该框架能有效刻画自动驾驶车云负载的时空特性，为自动驾驶应用的网络规划与资源管理提供了关键依据。

Abstract: Autonomous vehicles generate large volumes of data for applications such as fleet monitoring, model retraining, and high-definition map updates. Existing studies often rely on generic traffic traces, which do not capture the characteristics of autonomous driving workloads. This paper proposes a system-level workload modeling framework for vehicle-to-cloud data. We classify offloaded data into three types: telemetry, event-driven fleet learning, and high-definition map updates, while we model their generation using a parameterized formulation based on empirical data. Using a real-world mobility trace from Munich, we analyze the resulting workloads over time and space. The results show that workload scales with vehicle penetration, exhibits temporal structure and spatial imbalance across access points, and is distinguished from baseline traffic models.

</details>


### [177] [PNap: Lifecycle-aware Edge Multi-state sleep for Energy Efficient MEC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23323)
*Federico Giarrè,Holger Karl*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了PowerNap (PNap)框架，通过联合管理边缘服务器的睡眠状态和服务生命周期状态，在保证服务可用性的同时，显著降低了多接入边缘计算（MEC）系统的能耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MEC系统为了满足低延迟需求，需在多个位置维持边缘服务器运行，即使在低负载阶段也如此，导致能耗显著增加。现有的多级睡眠机制虽能节能，但与服务的非瞬时生命周期操作（如启动、停止）存在耦合冲突，如何在节能与服务连续性之间取得平衡成为亟待解决的问题。

Method: 引入PowerNap (PNap)，一种生命周期感知的编排框架。该方法利用流量预测技术，联合优化管理服务器的睡眠状态与服务的生命周期状态，旨在最小化活跃边缘服务器的数量并减少服务中断。

Result: 实验结果表明，与最先进的方法相比，PNap能够将能耗降低高达14.9%，同时在服务可用性方面达到与对比方案相当的水平。

Conclusion: PNap框架有效解决了MEC环境中能耗优化与服务连续性之间的矛盾，证明了生命周期感知的联合管理策略在降低能耗的同时能够维持高质量的服务可用性，为边缘计算资源管理提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Multi-access Edge Computings (MECs) enables low-latency services by executing applications at the network edge. To fulfill low-latency requirements of mobile users, providers have to keep multiple edge servers running at multiple locations, even when, in low-load phases, their capacity is not needed. This significantly increases energy consumption. Multi-state sleep mechanisms mitigate this issue by allowing servers to enter progressively deeper sleep states, trading energy savings for longer wake-up delays. At the same time, service execution depends on non-instantaneous lifecycle operations that cannot be performed while servers are asleep, tightly coupling energy management with service continuity. This paper introduces PowerNap (PNap), a lifecycle-aware orchestration framework that jointly manages server sleep states and service lifecycle states. By leveraging traffic forecasting, PNap jointly minimizes the number of active edge servers and service disruptions. We compare PNap against baselines approaches and a state-of-the-art approach. Results validate PNap, showing how it can reduce energy consumption by up to 14.9% with respect to a state-of-the-art solution while matching its service availability results.

</details>


### [178] [Can NR-V2X Sidelink support A2A links?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23330)
*Vittorio Todisco,Alessandro Bazzi*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文研究了将3GPP Release 18 Sidelink通信标准应用于空中实体间(A2A)链路的可行性，发现传播延迟是关键限制因素：距离超过42.4公里需要限制用户传输机会，但在此阈值以下无需修改标准即可实现通信。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着3GPP Release 18标准将Sidelink通信从车辆扩展到任何设备，研究其应用于空中实体间长距离(公里级)直接通信的可能性，以探索该标准在空对空场景下的适用性和局限性。

Method: 针对自主资源分配机制，分析Sidelink通信标准在长距离A2A链路中的应用，识别传播延迟等关键挑战，评估标准所需的修改和限制条件。

Result: 识别出传播延迟是关键限制因素。对于超过42.4公里的通信距离，需要限制用户的传输机会；而在该距离阈值以下，无需修改现有标准即可实现Sidelink通信。

Conclusion: Release 18 Sidelink通信标准可应用于距离42.4公里以下的空中实体间通信，无需标准修改；超过此距离需对传输机会进行限制，为A2A通信场景提供了明确的应用边界。

Abstract: In the context of 5G, 3GPP introduced New Radio vehicle to everything (NR-V2X) for direct vehicle-to-vehicle communication. However, starting from Release 18 the focus of the standard has been expanded from vehicles to any device and use case that can benefit from direct communication. In 3GPP terminology, the standard is now referred to simply as Sidelink communication. This standard allows direct communication between devices based on synchronous resource scheduling. Users can rely on controlled scheduling when in network coverage or, in the absence of coverage, autonomously select resources for transmission via a distributed resource allocation mechanism. Focusing on the autonomous resource allocation, this paper investigates the possibility of applying Release 18 Sidelink communication to Air-to-Air (A2A) links between airborne entities. The paper outlines the main challenges and required modifications to adapt the current standard for longer links in the order of kilometres. The analysis identifies the propagation delay as a critical limitation. Communications at distances over 42.4 km require a restriction of the user's transmitting opportunities. However, sidelink communication remains feasible for distances below this threshold without modifications to the standard.

</details>


### [179] [A Joint Reinforcement Learning Scheduling and Compression Framework for Teleoperated Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23387)
*Giacomo Avanzi,Marco Giordani,Michele Zorzi*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文针对6G网络中的远程驾驶场景，提出了集成强化学习方法来联合优化压缩与调度配置，并设计了元学习代理来动态选择最优部署策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 远程驾驶（TD）作为6G网络的关键应用，要求维持可靠且低延迟的通信。现有预测性服务质量（PQoS）机制虽能估计QoS降级并触发纠正措施，但需要针对TD场景优化压缩和调度配置的联合决策，以平衡传输效率与数据质量，同时最小化端到端延迟。

Method: 开发并实现了两个集成的强化学习代理：第一个代理确定TD传感器数据的最优压缩配置，平衡传输效率与数据质量；第二个代理确定最优调度配置，通过根据不同优先级分配无线资源来最小化端到端延迟。进一步提出了新的元学习代理，根据当前网络条件和应用需求动态选择集中式或分布式学习策略。

Result: 通过全栈ns-3仿真验证，集成代理在受限或拥塞网络中能够提供比仅优化压缩或调度的独立模型更优越的性能，证明了联合优化的有效性。

Conclusion: 联合优化压缩和调度配置的集成强化学习方法在远程驾驶场景中具有明显优势，而元学习代理的引入进一步提升了系统对动态网络条件的适应能力。

Abstract: Teleoperated driving (TD) is envisioned as a key application of future sixth generation (6G) networks. In this paradigm, connected vehicles transmit sensor-perception data to a remote (software) driver, which returns driving control commands to enhance traffic efficiency and road safety. This scenario imposes to maintain reliable and low-latency communication between the vehicle and the remote driver. To this aim, a promising solution is Predictive Quality of Service (PQoS), which provides mechanisms to estimate possible Quality of Service (QoS) degradation, and trigger timely network corrective actions accordingly. In particular, Reinforcement Learning (RL) agents can be trained to identify the optimal PQoS configuration. In this paper, we develop and implement two integrated RL agents that jointly determine (i) the optimal compression configuration for TD sensor data to balance the trade-off between transmission efficiency and data quality, and (ii) the optimal scheduling configuration to minimize the end-to-end latency by allocating radio resources according to different priority levels. We prove via full-stack ns-3 simulations that our integrated agents can deliver superior performance than any standalone model that only optimizes either compression or scheduling, especially in constrained or congested networks. While these agents can be deployed using either centralized or decentralized learning, we further propose a new meta-learning agent that dynamically selects the most appropriate strategy between the two based on current network conditions and application requirements.

</details>


<div id='cs.MA'></div>

# cs.MA [[Back]](#toc)

### [180] [Planning over MAPF Agent Dependencies via Multi-Dependency PIBT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23405)
*Zixiang Jiang,Yulun Zhang,Rishi Veerapaneni,Jiaoyang Li*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出MD-PIBT框架，通过规划代理依赖关系来解决MAPF问题，可复现PIBT/EPIBT并支持新的规划策略，能有效处理多达10,000个代理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有PIBT及其扩展EPIBT算法受限于基于规则的规划过程，搜索范围仅限于最多与一个其他代理冲突的路径，缺乏通用性，无法满足大规模多代理路径规划的需求。

Method: 受PIBT优先继承逻辑启发，定义代理依赖概念，提出多依赖PIBT（MD-PIBT）框架，在代理依赖关系上进行搜索；该框架可通过特定参数化复现PIBT和EPIBT，同时支持生成新的规划策略。

Result: 实验表明MD-PIBT能有效规划多达10,000个同构代理，支持多种运动学约束（包括pebble motion、旋转运动、具有速度和加速度限制的差分驱动机器人），在大型代理MAPF问题上表现尤为出色。

Conclusion: MD-PIBT为大规模多代理路径规划提供了更通用的解决方案，突破了原有算法的局限性，在不同MAPF变体上展现出优异性能。

Abstract: Modern Multi-Agent Path Finding (MAPF) algorithms must plan for hundreds to thousands of agents in congested environments within a second, requiring highly efficient algorithms. Priority Inheritance with Backtracking (PIBT) is a popular algorithm capable of effectively planning in such situations. However, PIBT is constrained by its rule-based planning procedure and lacks generality because it restricts its search to paths that conflict with at most one other agent. This limitation also applies to Enhanced PIBT (EPIBT), a recent extension of PIBT. In this paper, we describe a new perspective on solving MAPF by planning over agent dependencies. Taking inspiration from PIBT's priority inheritance logic, we define the concept of agent dependencies and propose Multi-Dependency PIBT (MD-PIBT) that searches over agent dependencies. MD-PIBT is a general framework where specific parameterizations can reproduce PIBT and EPIBT. At the same time, alternative configurations yield novel planning strategies that are not expressible by PIBT or EPIBT. Our experiments demonstrate that MD-PIBT effectively plans for as many as 10,000 homogeneous agents under various kinodynamic constraints, including pebble motion, rotation motion, and differential drive robots with speed and acceleration limits. We perform thorough evaluations on different variants of MAPF and find that MD-PIBT is particularly effective in MAPF with large agents.

</details>
